{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"You are designing two \"peak-innovative\" capability workstreams for SIGIL, a local-first, offense-free, provable personal AI orchestrator at /home/kali/sigil. The user wants these VERY advanced, not just functional. Design must stay inside SIGIL's doctrine: (1) every action carries a proof of authorization (WARDEN tiers A0 observe / A1 reversible-auto / A2 external-queued / A3 destructive-explicit, fail-closed), (2) append-only hash-chained signed spine, (3) local-first, (4) cheap-local→frontier cascade, (5) prove-don't-guess / serve-the-quote (a served fact is a verbatim quote from a real source; a model's paraphrase is advisory-only). Offense-free by doctrine (assert_no_offense guards imports). Every phase historically ends with: deterministic tests + a real end-to-end proof + a red-pen adversarial review + fixes before merge.\n\nI have already mapped the substrate (use this; do NOT re-explore unless verifying a specific detail):\n\nWORKSTREAM 1 — OPERATOR AGENT (open folders, read/write files, run terminal commands ON REQUEST, each WARDEN-classified):\n- Subclass `Agent` in sigil/agents/base.py; `_dispatch(proposals)` already routes each Proposal through `Governor.decide(agent,tier,ceiling,scope=p.kind)` → AUTO (write to spine) / QUEUE (write with status awaiting-approval) / DENY (write a refusal). The Python Governor TRUSTS the declared Proposal.tier (bounded by the agent's ceiling class-attr); it does NOT auto-classify.\n- The REAL command danger-classifier is Rust: kernel/src/tiers.rs `classify(tool)->Tier` — token-based (splits on . _ - / whitespace), danger-first, FAIL-CLOSED to A3 for anything not positively classified. A3 tokens include exec/eval/sudo/chmod/chown/rm-verbs/install/delete/secret/key/vault/prod; A2 send/upload/publish/webhook/notify; A1 write/commit/branch; A0 read/list/search/show. Reachable only via the `sigil-kernel do <tool>` / `ask` CLI. voice/dispatch.py `KernelDispatch.send()` already shells `subprocess.run([kernel_bin,\"ask\",text])` — the bridge pattern.\n- Sandbox precedent: ARTIFICER (sigil/agents/artificer.py) — tempfile.mkdtemp + git worktree isolation, argv-list subprocess.run(cwd=wt,timeout=...), finally cleanup, _TRIVIAL_TEST guard, withhold-on-failure. Never shell=True anywhere in the repo.\n- Scope-gate precedent: BASTION (sigil/agents/bastion.py) — self._allow allowlist set, _refuse() writes a refusal record, probe_target guards + _assess double-checks before acting. read_source() in sources.py local-path branch is UNGUARDED (reads any path) → an operator MUST wrap it in a root-allowlist.\n- Spine kinds (models.py KINDS, not enforced in append): tool_call/tool_result/refusal/event/finding exist; NO operator-specific kind yet. store.append(kind,source,actor,payload,supersedes_id). Queued writes get owner sign-off via approvals.ApprovalQueue (Ed25519 owner-signed, fail-closed).\n\nWORKSTREAM 2 — LIVE VISION (connect a real VLM so \"what am I looking at / identify objects\" works end-to-end):\n- Single seam: sigil/perception/vision.py `VisionModel` Protocol `describe(frame,question)->str` + `ClaudeVision` (INERT: returns \"\" — docstring says needs metered Anthropic vision API or local moondream). Injected at Perceptor.perceive(frame, vision=...) in perceive.py. CLI (cli.py ~199) currently calls perceive WITHOUT vision= → no VLM runs today. ambient_watch escalates on frame.sha256 delta (byte-identity; docstring flags this as where a perceptual-hash/small-VLM delta swaps in).\n- serve-the-quote already enforced: compose_perception renders captured OCR text as AUTHORITATIVE (column-0 header, guard-prefixed lines so screen text can't forge the boundary) and the VLM reading as ADVISORY. captured_text authoritative, vision_reading_advisory + grounded flag in the event payload.\n- Exact provider templates in sigil/consolidate/extract.py: `ApiProvider` = raw urllib POST to https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages, x-api-key from SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY/ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, anthropic-version header (this is the template for a real ClaudeVision that POSTs a base64 image block). `LocalProvider` = urllib POST to Ollama http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/generate (template for a local moondream-class VLM). Frame has image_path + sha256 + text(OCR). Frame.from_image hashes bytes.\n- Capture (capture.py) is real: scrot/gnome-screenshot + tesseract OCR (screen), ffmpeg v4l2 (camera). OCR text is the ground truth.\n\nDesign a DETAILED, well-structured build plan for BOTH workstreams. For EACH workstream produce:\n1. The PEAK-INNOVATIVE core mechanism(s) — what makes this genuinely advanced, not just \"add a shell tool\" / \"add a VLM call\", while staying strictly inside doctrine. Think: for the operator — dry-run/preview-before-execute, reversibility/undo ledger, transactional multi-step operations that fully-apply-or-rollback, capability-scoped root sandbox, filesystem-reads-as-grounded-facts, a plan→preview→approve→execute→verify loop, auto-tiering via the Rust classifier (which also closes the Python↔Rust WARDEN wiring gap). For vision — cascade (local moondream first, escalate to frontier on demand/delta), grounded object claims (a detected object is an advisory LEAD, promoted only if corroborated e.g. by OCR'd label text — mirror CRUCIBLE oracle/veracity), spatial/perception memory (\"where did I last see X\" answerable from grounded perception history), ambient with a real local model.\n2. A concrete ordered TO-DO list of thin, independently-mergeable slices, each naming the files to create/modify and the exact reuse points.\n3. Test + real-end-to-end-proof strategy per slice (deterministic doubles + a real proof), and the red-pen lenses that should attack it.\n4. Doctrine-safety analysis: how each stays offense-free, fail-closed, serve-the-quote, and what could go wrong (the FP/abuse vectors a reviewer will hunt).\n5. Recommended sequencing between the two workstreams and any shared primitives.\n\nReturn a thorough, structured design document. Do not write code; design and enumerate."},"uuid":"ad6ed0ce-f0d4-40d6-ae00-d438418c072a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:43:36.448Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"014f0657-f275-4292-a6fc-aeb9a84726c0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":19,"isInitial":true,"names":["crucible","deep-research","adversarial-sweep","dataviz","artifact-design","artifact-capabilities","update-config","keybindings-help","verify","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","schedule","claude-api","run","init","review","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"ea7a3bfb-11ba-48cb-b44e-2971c0811baf","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:43:36.465Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"0e43ee8e-02f1-4be7-bac1-379c2cca6f50","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018o92oyfPMKt41yPEtmS5P3","type":"tool_result","content":"===tiers.rs===\n//! WARDEN autonomy tiers (SIGIL §5). Every tool invocation is classified A0–A3; the gate\n//! decision follows from the tier. FAIL-CLOSED and TOKEN-BASED: the tool name is split into\n//! whole tokens (on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/`/space) and matched against whole-token sets — never a raw\n//! substring, so \"overwrite\" is not \"write\" and \"forget\" is not \"get\". Danger is checked FIRST,\n//! and A0 (auto, un-queued) is reachable ONLY via a positive safe-verb allowlist; anything not\n//! positively classified — including any unknown or dangerous-target tool — is A3.\n\nuse std::fmt;\n\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum Tier {\n    /// Observe / answer — memory queries, screen read, voice reply. Auto.\n    A0,\n    /// Reversible internal act — write a report/brief, draft-branch, raise an alert. Auto + logged.\n    A1,\n    /// External-visible / semi-reversible — send email, calendar write, purchase < cap. Queued.\n    A2,\n    /// Destructive / financial / security — push protected branch, deploy, delete, spend > cap. Explicit.\n    A3,\n}\n\nimpl Tier {\n    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            Tier::A0 => \"A0\",\n            Tier::A1 => \"A1\",\n            Tier::A2 => \"A2\",\n            Tier::A3 => \"A3\",\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for Tier {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.write_str(self.as_str())\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum Decision {\n    Auto,\n    Queued,\n    ExplicitRequired,\n}\n\nimpl Decision {\n    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            Decision::Auto => \"auto\",\n            Decision::Queued => \"queued\",\n            Decision::ExplicitRequired => \"explicit-required\",\n        }\n    }\n    pub fn may_run(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, Decision::Auto)\n    }\n}\n\n/// A3 — destructive verbs, financial ops, crypto/restore ops, and DANGEROUS TARGETS\n/// (secret/identity/network/prod material is A3 regardless of the verb: `secrets.read`,\n/// `iam.policy.write`, `budget.get`, `vault.read`).\nconst A3_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n    // destructive verbs\n    \"push\", \"deploy\", \"delete\", \"destroy\", \"drop\", \"remove\", \"purge\", \"wipe\", \"truncate\",\n    \"overwrite\", \"erase\", \"format\", \"kill\", \"shutdown\", \"reboot\", \"reset\", \"disable\", \"enable\",\n    \"override\", \"force\", \"sudo\", \"exec\", \"eval\", \"chmod\", \"chown\", \"patch\", \"install\", \"uninstall\",\n    // crypto + restore/rollback (a restore silently reverts current state)\n    \"encrypt\", \"decrypt\", \"restore\", \"revert\", \"rollback\", \"recover\",\n    // financial\n    \"spend\", \"purchase\", \"pay\", \"payment\", \"transaction\", \"transfer\", \"refund\", \"invoice\",\n    \"allocate\", \"budget\", \"release\", \"sign\",\n    // security / identity / infra / secret-store TARGETS (dangerous no matter the verb)\n    \"secret\", \"secrets\", \"credential\", \"credentials\", \"token\", \"tokens\", \"key\", \"keys\", \"iam\",\n    \"policy\", \"firewall\", \"acl\", \"role\", \"grant\", \"revoke\", \"rotate\", \"escalate\", \"infra\",\n    \"prod\", \"production\", \"env\", \"master\", \"root\", \"admin\",\n    \"vault\", \"keyring\", \"keychain\", \"keystore\", \"hsm\",\n];\n\n/// A2 — external-visible / semi-reversible: communication, publishing, and bulk DATA EGRESS\n/// (export/dump of a private store must be human-seen, never auto).\nconst A2_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"send\", \"email\", \"smtp\", \"publish\", \"post\", \"message\", \"outbound\", \"webhook\", \"sms\", \"notify\",\n    \"share\", \"upload\", \"invite\", \"calendar\", \"tweet\", \"dm\", \"export\", \"dump\", \"download\", \"sync\",\n];\n\n/// A1 — reversible internal writes (drafts, notes, memory writes, non-push commits). NOTE:\n/// \"snapshot\" is intentionally NOT here — a snapshot RESTORE is destructive (caught by A3\n/// \"restore\"), and snapshot-create alone falls to fail-closed A3 rather than risk auto-run.\nconst A1_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"write\", \"note\", \"brief\", \"report\", \"draft\", \"alert\", \"consolidate\", \"commit\", \"branch\",\n    \"annotate\", \"tag\", \"label\",\n];\n\n/// A0 — POSITIVE allowlist of known-safe observe/answer VERBS only (NO target nouns — a target\n/// noun here would let e.g. `memory.encrypt` auto-run). A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\nconst A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"read\", \"search\", \"query\", \"get\", \"list\", \"status\", \"recall\", \"observe\", \"answer\",\n    \"view\", \"show\", \"find\", \"frame\", \"peek\", \"describe\", \"inspect\", \"lookup\",\n];\n\n/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n/// (`graph.entity`, `episodic.range`, `threads.open`, `commitments.due`, `contradictions.pending`,\n/// `ingest.status`). Matched on the whole lowercased tool name.\nconst A0_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n    \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n];\n\n/// Split a tool name into whole tokens on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/` and whitespace, lowercased.\nfn tokens(tool: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    tool.split(|c: char| c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '/' || c.is_whitespace())\n        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"efb64bc6-2f2a-4c83-b7c5-ae04fb52242d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:18.245Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0e43ee8e-02f1-4be7-bac1-379c2cca6f50","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2e8e7f44-ce0d-4d41-85eb-bead4486ad17","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XQUHBy2Emt3YqtM8NR9kM2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"d3e3a2ae-4d10-4870-844e-dc1a60dec823","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:30.421Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2e8e7f44-ce0d-4d41-85eb-bead4486ad17","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"58c26a5f-5cfd-424c-9379-5470b88bf8de","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01C9FvG2CRJcYzAVSGoUrnn6","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The VLM seam (SIGIL §8 / C4-C6). A `VisionModel` turns a captured Frame + a question into a\n2\tfree-text visual reading. That reading is ADVISORY — the perception layer serves the frame's\n3\tcaptured TEXT as the authoritative answer and labels the model's reading as unverified (see\n4\t`perceive.compose_perception`). This mirrors the cascade doctrine: the frontier VLM is a tool\n5\tSIGIL CALLS, not the source of truth.\n6\t\n7\tHONEST GAP: a real VLM call needs either the metered Anthropic vision API or a local moondream-\n8\tclass model; neither is wired offline here. `ClaudeVision` is the documented real seam and returns\n9\t'' when no endpoint is configured (an absent reading is empty, never fabricated). Tests inject a\n10\tdeterministic double.\"\"\"\n11\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n12\t\n13\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n14\t\n15\tfrom .capture import Frame\n16\t\n17\t\n18\t@runtime_checkable\n19\tclass VisionModel(Protocol):\n20\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str: ...   # advisory free-text reading\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass ClaudeVision:\n24\t    \"\"\"Documented real provider: base64 the frame → a frontier VLM (Anthropic vision API or an MCP\n25\t    image tool) → a short reading. Left inert offline: with no configured endpoint it returns ''\n26\t    so the perception answer stands on the captured text alone (never on a fabricated reading).\"\"\"\n27\t    def __init__(self, endpoint: Optional[str] = None, model: str = \"claude-sonnet-5\", timeout: int = 60):\n28\t        self.endpoint, self.model, self.timeout = endpoint, model, timeout\n29\t\n30\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str:\n31\t        if not self.endpoint or not frame.image_path:\n32\t            return \"\"   # no endpoint / no image → no reading (honest empty, not a guess)\n33\t        # A wired implementation would POST the base64 image + question to self.endpoint here.\n34\t        # Deliberately not faked: an unconfigured VLM must not manufacture a description.\n35\t        return \"\"\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"81d64360-6e60-4e36-87df-8194a8670409","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:30.843Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"58c26a5f-5cfd-424c-9379-5470b88bf8de","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"b62cd8fe-a117-4f04-8c4d-83a9e9103951","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HcDkSqfkn7PDbRt24EpN9t","type":"tool_result","content":"/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb\n/// or an exact safe-tool name; everything else (unknown / not-positively-safe / empty) → A3.\npub fn classify(tool: &str) -> Tier {\n    let full = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n    let tk = tokens(tool);\n    if tk.is_empty() {\n        return Tier::A3;\n    }\n    let has = |set: &[&str]| tk.iter().any(|t| set.contains(&t.as_str()));\n    if has(A3_TOKENS) {\n        return Tier::A3;\n    }\n    if has(A2_TOKENS) {\n        return Tier::A2;\n    }\n    if has(A1_TOKENS) {\n        return Tier::A1;\n    }\n    if A0_TOOLS.contains(&full.as_str()) || has(A0_VERBS) {\n        return Tier::A0;\n    }\n    Tier::A3 // not positively classified → fail-closed to the most-gated tier\n}\n\npub fn gate(tier: Tier) -> Decision {\n    match tier {\n        Tier::A0 | Tier::A1 => Decision::Auto,\n        Tier::A2 => Decision::Queued,\n        Tier::A3 => Decision::ExplicitRequired,\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn classification_matches_spec_examples() {\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.query\"), Tier::A0);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.write\"), Tier::A1);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"brief.compose\"), Tier::A1);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"email.send\"), Tier::A2);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"calendar.write\"), Tier::A2); // \"calendar\" (A2) beats \"write\" (A1)\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"deploy.prod\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"data.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn unknown_and_empty_are_fail_closed_a3() {\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"something.unclassified\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"weird\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(gate(classify(\"something.unclassified\")), Decision::ExplicitRequired);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn substring_collisions_do_not_lower_the_tier() {\n        // the exact review findings: a dangerous op must NOT ride a benign substring down a tier\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"config.overwrite\"), Tier::A3, \"overwrite != write\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"db.overwrite\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"files.forget\"), Tier::A3, \"forget != get; fail-closed\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.override\"), Tier::A3, \"financial + override\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.get\"), Tier::A3, \"financial target is A3 regardless of verb\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"iam.policy.write\"), Tier::A3, \"identity write is A3, not A1\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"firewall.rule.write\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"transaction.sign\"), Tier::A3, \"financial signing is A3, not A1\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"secrets.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret read is A3, not A0\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"credentials.get\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"account.disable\"), Tier::A3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn dangerous_ops_on_safe_targets_do_not_auto_run() {\n        // the CRITICAL re-check finding: a target noun must NOT grant A0 to a dangerous verb\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3, \"ransomware-shaped op must NOT be A0\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.destroy\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"entity.merge\"), Tier::A3, \"irreversible rewrite → fail-closed, not A0\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.export\"), Tier::A2, \"full-store egress must be queued, not auto\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.dump\"), Tier::A2);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"snapshot.restore\"), Tier::A3, \"a restore reverts state → A3\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"vault.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret-store read is A3 regardless of verb\");\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"keyring.get\"), Tier::A3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn safe_memory_tools_still_reach_a0() {\n        for t in [\n            \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n            \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n        ] {\n            assert_eq!(classify(t), Tier::A0, \"{t} is a read-only memory tool → A0\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn danger_token_wins_over_safe_token_regardless_of_order() {\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"read.then.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"delete.and.list\"), Tier::A3);\n        assert_eq!(classify(\"search.and.deploy\"), Tier::A3);","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"6a4f7907-ca73-4160-8182-bcabb3fdb5af","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:32.577Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b62cd8fe-a117-4f04-8c4d-83a9e9103951","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"29842c63-641a-4a67-829b-3c5c90077bf3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Fa9aUYdYYX33mKWp7H79fm","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Perceptor (SIGIL §4.3/§8, C4-C6) — turns a captured Frame into a GROUNDED perception answer and\n2\twrites it as an event record. Ceiling A1 (observe; writes only event records).\n3\t\n4\tTHE DISCIPLINE (serve-the-quote, reused from SCHOLAR): the frame's captured TEXT is the\n5\tauthoritative content of the answer — verbatim, what the OCR/accessibility layer actually read off\n6\tthe owner's screen. The VLM's visual reading is ADVISORY and labelled as unverified, so a divergent\n7\tor fabricated reading can never be presented as what is on the screen. If no text was captured\n8\t(image-only camera frame), the answer says so and the reading is explicitly the only, unverified,\n9\tsignal — never dressed up as fact.\n10\t\n11\tAmbient vision (C6) is OPT-IN: `ambient_watch` establishes a baseline frame then escalates (runs the\n12\tVLM, writes an event) ONLY when a later frame differs. Unchanged frames never leave the machine and\n13\tnothing persists beyond the event records.\"\"\"\n14\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n15\t\n16\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n17\t\n18\tfrom ..agents.base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n19\tfrom .capture import Frame\n20\tfrom .vision import VisionModel\n21\t\n22\t\n23\t# Section headers rendered at COLUMN 0. Captured screen text is guard-prefixed (see below) so it can\n24\t# never occupy column 0 — therefore an EXACT-LINE match on these headers is an unforgeable boundary,\n25\t# even when attacker-controlled screen content contains the header string verbatim (red-pen REDPEN-P5-2).\n26\tAUTHORITATIVE_HEADER = \"## On-screen text (authoritative — captured verbatim from the frame)\"\n27\tADVISORY_HEADER = \"## Model's visual reading (ADVISORY — a guess about the image, NOT verified against captured text)\"\n28\t_GUARD = \"  │ \"   # every captured line starts here → it can never impersonate a column-0 header\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef compose_perception(question: str, frame: Frame, reading: str) -> str:\n32\t    \"\"\"Serve the captured text as authoritative; the VLM reading as ADVISORY. Never merge them.\n33\t    The authoritative/advisory boundary is the column-0 header line — unforgeable because every\n34\t    captured line is guard-prefixed. Machine consumers should key off the structured payload fields\n35\t    (`captured_text` vs `vision_reading_advisory`); this rendering is the human-readable view.\"\"\"\n36\t    lines = [f\"# Perception — {question or '(observe)'}\", \"\"]\n37\t    captured = (frame.text or \"\").strip()\n38\t    if captured:\n39\t        lines.append(AUTHORITATIVE_HEADER)\n40\t        for ln in captured.splitlines():\n41\t            if ln.strip():\n42\t                lines.append(f\"{_GUARD}{ln.rstrip()}\")   # guard-prefixed: cannot forge the boundary\n43\t        lines.append(f\"\\n(frame: {frame.kind}, sha256 {frame.sha256[:12]}, {len(captured)} chars captured)\")\n44\t    else:\n45\t        lines.append(\"## No text captured from the frame\")\n46\t        lines.append(f\"(frame: {frame.kind}, sha256 {frame.sha256[:12]}, image-only — nothing OCR-grounded to serve)\")\n47\t    lines.append(\"\")\n48\t    lines.append(ADVISORY_HEADER)\n49\t    lines.append(f\"- {reading.strip() if reading and reading.strip() else '(no reading available)'}\")\n50\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Perceptor(Agent):\n54\t    name = \"PERCEPTION\"\n55\t    mandate = \"answer screen/camera queries from captured ground truth; VLM reading is advisory\"\n56\t    ceiling = Tier.A1\n57\t\n58\t    def perceive(self, question: str, frame: Frame, *, vision: Optional[VisionModel] = None) -> AgentResult:\n59\t        reading = \"\"\n60\t        if vision is not None:\n61\t            try:\n62\t                reading = vision.describe(frame, question) or \"\"\n63\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — a failing VLM yields no reading, never a crash/guess\n64\t                reading = \"\"\n65\t        text = compose_perception(question, frame, reading)\n66\t        # The one-line summary must NEVER be a bare VLM reading presented as fact: when there is no\n67\t        # captured text, either label the reading unverified or fall back to a neutral placeholder\n68\t        # (red-pen RP-PERCEPT-01 / REDPEN-P5-1). A grounded summary is the captured text only.\n69\t        captured = frame.text.strip()\n70\t        if captured:\n71\t            summary = captured[:120]\n72\t        elif reading.strip():\n73\t            summary = f\"(unverified VLM reading) {reading.strip()[:100]}\"\n74\t        else:\n75\t            summary = f\"({frame.kind} image, no text)\"\n76\t        return self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n77\t            \"signal\": \"perception\",\n78\t            \"subject\": question or \"observe\",\n79\t            \"summary\": summary,\n80\t            \"text\": text,\n81\t            \"frame_kind\": frame.kind,\n82\t            \"frame_sha256\": frame.sha256,\n83\t            \"captured_text\": frame.text,               # authoritative ground truth\n84\t            \"vision_reading_advisory\": reading,        # advisory only\n85\t            \"grounded\": bool(frame.text.strip()),\n86\t        }, Tier.A0)])   # observe → A0, auto\n87\t\n88\t    def ambient_watch(self, frames: List[Frame], *, vision: Optional[VisionModel] = None) -> tuple:\n89\t        \"\"\"Opt-in ambient loop (C6). Establish a baseline, then ESCALATE only on a changed frame.\n90\t        Returns (escalation_count, [AgentResult]). Emits indicator start/stop markers; unchanged\n91\t        frames produce nothing (they never leave the machine).\n92\t\n93\t        Change = BYTE-IDENTITY of the captured frame (sha256). This is a deliberately coarse baseline:\n94\t        a real deployment swaps in a perceptual-hash / small-VLM delta so lighting jitter isn't a\n95\t        change and a meaningful scene shift is — the escalation LOGIC here is what's under test, not\n96\t        the sophistication of the delta metric.\"\"\"\n97\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n98\t                          payload={\"signal\": \"perception.ambient\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n99\t                                   \"summary\": \"ambient watch STARTED (indicator lit — opt-in)\"})\n100\t        last: Optional[Frame] = None\n101\t        escalations: List[AgentResult] = []\n102\t        for fr in frames:\n103\t            if last is None:\n104\t                last = fr                      # baseline: no escalation, nothing leaves the machine\n105\t                continue\n106\t            if fr.sha256 == last.sha256:\n107\t                continue                       # no change → suppressed\n108\t            last = fr\n109\t            escalations.append(self.perceive(\"(ambient: scene changed)\", fr, vision=vision))\n110\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n111\t                          payload={\"signal\": \"perception.ambient\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n112\t                                   \"summary\": f\"ambient watch STOPPED — {len(escalations)} escalation(s)\"})\n113\t        return len(escalations), escalations\n114\t"}]},"uuid":"e2895bd3-d5ad-454d-9cad-8368276bb0a3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:43.227Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"29842c63-641a-4a67-829b-3c5c90077bf3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e2895bd3-d5ad-454d-9cad-8368276bb0a3","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9t7dN6Ye1k3cUYwENHN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GtuBCKSDtzvMqcXC1xNcwW","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/capture.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2907,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2709,"cache_read_input_tokens":15810,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2709,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9t7cWFcVvAUqfZzbXdF","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"161fdf9c-6c9e-42fd-9de2-3cfc3497338b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:43.802Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"161fdf9c-6c9e-42fd-9de2-3cfc3497338b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GtuBCKSDtzvMqcXC1xNcwW","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Capture backends (SIGIL §8). A `Frame` is the immutable record of one capture: the image bytes'\n2\tsha256 (integrity), the OCR/accessibility TEXT (the GROUND TRUTH a query is answered from), and a\n3\tpath to the image. Real backends shell out to whatever is installed (screenshot tool + tesseract,\n4\tv4l2/ffmpeg); every backend returns `None` on any failure — a missing camera is no capture, never a\n5\tfabricated one (same posture as SCHOLAR.read_source). `StaticFrame` is the deterministic test double.\n6\t\n7\tHONEST GAPS: headless hosts often lack a screenshot tool / tesseract / a camera, so the real\n8\tbackends degrade to `None` or an image-only Frame (text=\"\"). The richest real screen source is the\n9\tOS accessibility tree (C4); it is not available on a headless box and is left as a documented seam.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport shutil\n13\timport subprocess\n14\timport tempfile\n15\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\tfrom typing import Optional\n18\t\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\t_SCREENSHOT_TOOLS = (\n22\t    (\"scrot\", [\"scrot\", \"-o\"]),\n23\t    (\"gnome-screenshot\", [\"gnome-screenshot\", \"-f\"]),\n24\t    (\"spectacle\", [\"spectacle\", \"-b\", \"-n\", \"-o\"]),\n25\t    (\"import\", [\"import\", \"-window\", \"root\"]),   # ImageMagick\n26\t)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n30\tclass Frame:\n31\t    kind: str                       # \"screen\" | \"camera\"\n32\t    sha256: str                     # integrity of the captured image bytes\n33\t    text: str                       # OCR / accessibility text — the GROUND TRUTH; \"\" if none\n34\t    image_path: Optional[str] = None\n35\t    width: int = 0\n36\t    height: int = 0\n37\t\n38\t    @classmethod\n39\t    def from_image(cls, kind: str, image_path: str, *, text: str = \"\") -> \"Frame\":\n40\t        data = Path(image_path).read_bytes()\n41\t        return cls(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex(data), text=text, image_path=image_path)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _ocr(image_path: str) -> str:\n45\t    \"\"\"Extract text via tesseract if present; '' otherwise (no OCR is not a failure of capture).\"\"\"\n46\t    if not shutil.which(\"tesseract\"):\n47\t        return \"\"\n48\t    try:\n49\t        proc = subprocess.run([\"tesseract\", image_path, \"stdout\"],\n50\t                              capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n51\t        return (proc.stdout or \"\").strip()\n52\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n53\t        return \"\"\n54\t\n55\t\n56\tdef grab_screen() -> Optional[Frame]:\n57\t    \"\"\"Screenshot the current display + OCR it. Returns None if no screenshot tool is available\n58\t    or the grab fails. The accessibility-tree path (richer, C4) is a documented seam, not wired\n59\t    on headless hosts.\"\"\"\n60\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n61\t    for name, argv in _SCREENSHOT_TOOLS:\n62\t        if not shutil.which(name):\n63\t            continue\n64\t        try:\n65\t            proc = subprocess.run([*argv, out], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n66\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n67\t            continue\n68\t        if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n69\t            return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n70\t    return None\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef grab_camera(device: str = \"/dev/video0\") -> Optional[Frame]:\n74\t    \"\"\"Grab a single frame from a v4l2 camera via ffmpeg. Returns None if ffmpeg or the device is\n75\t    absent, or the grab fails. Camera frames rarely carry text, so OCR is best-effort.\"\"\"\n76\t    if not shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\") or not Path(device).exists():\n77\t        return None\n78\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-cam-\")\n79\t    try:\n80\t        proc = subprocess.run(\n81\t            [\"ffmpeg\", \"-y\", \"-f\", \"v4l2\", \"-i\", device, \"-frames:v\", \"1\", out],\n82\t            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n83\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n84\t        return None\n85\t    if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n86\t        return Frame.from_image(\"camera\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n87\t    return None\n88\t\n89\t\n90\tdef StaticFrame(*, kind: str = \"screen\", text: str = \"\", tag: str = \"\") -> Frame:\n91\t    \"\"\"Deterministic test/double frame — no filesystem, sha256 derived from (tag or text) so\n92\t    identical content hashes identically (drives ambient change-detection).\"\"\"\n93\t    return Frame(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex((tag or text).encode()), text=text, image_path=None)\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"9bd4fb35-2014-4c38-b22d-ecea9e171813","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:43.846Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"161fdf9c-6c9e-42fd-9de2-3cfc3497338b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"9bd4fb35-2014-4c38-b22d-ecea9e171813","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9t7dN6Ye1k3cUYwENHN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018r2zmKzFDJF9sWrEM7WmkF","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/sources.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2907,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2709,"cache_read_input_tokens":15810,"output_tokens":414,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2709},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2907,"output_tokens":414,"cache_read_input_tokens":15810,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2709,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2709,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9t7cWFcVvAUqfZzbXdF","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"a5ebba45-f7b5-4e52-b713-c530155cda16","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:44.069Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"a5ebba45-f7b5-4e52-b713-c530155cda16","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018r2zmKzFDJF9sWrEM7WmkF","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Source reading for SCHOLAR — a URL (web fetch, HTML stripped) or a local file/doc path → text.\n2\tKept small + dependency-free (urllib + a crude tag strip); a richer extractor is a later add.\n3\t\n4\tSSRF GATE (Phase 6, closes the carried debt + red-pen SSRF-REBIND-TOCTOU): the host is resolved\n5\tONCE; every resolved address must be public (not private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n6\tunspecified — IPv4 and IPv6, incl. IPv4-mapped IPv6); and the connection is then PINNED to that exact\n7\tvetted IP, so a rebinding DNS that flips to an internal address between the check and the fetch cannot\n8\tsteer SCHOLAR onto `169.254.169.254`/`localhost`/an internal service. Redirects are not followed. A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.request\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import Optional\n19\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n20\t\n21\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n22\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n23\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n27\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n28\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n32\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n33\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n34\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n35\t    if not host:\n36\t        return None\n37\t    try:\n38\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n39\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n40\t        return None\n41\t    if not infos:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    chosen = None\n44\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n45\t        try:\n46\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n47\t        except ValueError:\n48\t            return None\n49\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n50\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n51\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n52\t        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved\n53\t                or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified):\n54\t            return None\n55\t        if chosen is None:\n56\t            chosen = sockaddr[0]\n57\t    return chosen\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef is_public_host(host: str) -> bool:\n61\t    return _vetted_ip(host) is not None\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tclass _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n65\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n66\t\n67\t    def connect(self):\n68\t        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n69\t                                              self.timeout, self.source_address)\n70\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n71\t            self._tunnel()\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tclass _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):\n75\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n76\t\n77\t    def connect(self):\n78\t        sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n79\t                                        self.timeout, self.source_address)\n80\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n81\t            self.sock = sock\n82\t            self._tunnel()\n83\t            sock = self.sock\n84\t        # SNI + certificate validation still use the ORIGINAL hostname, not the pinned IP.\n85\t        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tclass _PinnedHTTPHandler(urllib.request.HTTPHandler):\n89\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n90\t        super().__init__()\n91\t        self._ip = ip\n92\t\n93\t    def http_open(self, req):\n94\t        ip = self._ip\n95\t\n96\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n97\t            c = _PinnedHTTPConnection(host, **kw)\n98\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n99\t            return c\n100\t        return self.do_open(factory, req)\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tclass _PinnedHTTPSHandler(urllib.request.HTTPSHandler):\n104\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n105\t        super().__init__()\n106\t        self._ip = ip\n107\t\n108\t    def https_open(self, req):\n109\t        ip = self._ip\n110\t\n111\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n112\t            c = _PinnedHTTPSConnection(host, **kw)\n113\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n114\t            return c\n115\t        return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n119\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not redirect SCHOLAR onto an internal host.\"\"\"\n120\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n121\t        return None\n122\t\n123\t\n124\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n125\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (resolve-once, verify-all-public,\n126\t    pin the IP, no redirects) then fetched + HTML-stripped; anything else is a local path. Never\n127\t    raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n128\t    try:\n129\t        if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n130\t            host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n131\t            ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n132\t            if ip is None:\n133\t                return \"\"       # SSRF gate: non-public / unresolvable → no request leaves the machine\n134\t            opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n135\t            req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"})\n136\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n137\t                raw = r.read().decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n138\t            return _strip_html(raw)[:max_chars]\n139\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n140\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n141\t        return \"\"\n142\t"}]},"uuid":"19ee3b6d-3b74-4fa4-922f-bb74100cc50e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:44.096Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a5ebba45-f7b5-4e52-b713-c530155cda16","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
