{"parentUuid":"5d036404-45af-4548-9c59-1c786376d2cf","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UqMtUH8tdwZKAcQoTYskuX","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ARTIFICER (SIGIL §4.4) — engineering: drives headless Claude Code against a repo to own a\n2\tcoding task end to end. Ceiling A2 (a PR ready-for-review queues for approval); `git push` to a\n3\tprotected branch, deploys, and dependency additions are A3 — ARTIFICER NEVER pushes.\n4\t\n5\tCorrectness discipline (the §4.4 focus): it runs the tests BEFORE claiming done — a PR is\n6\tproposed ONLY if the test command passes on the change; a failing change is reported, not\n7\tshipped. The result is a local branch + a plain-language diff summary, QUEUED for the human to\n8\treview and push. The coder is pluggable: `ClaudeCoder` (headless `claude -p`) or a deterministic\n9\ttest double.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport re\n13\timport shutil\n14\timport subprocess\n15\timport tempfile\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n18\t\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n21\t\n22\t# commands that \"pass\" trivially — a PR must never claim tests passing on one of these.\n23\t_TRIVIAL_TEST = frozenset({(), (\"true\",), (\":\",), (\"/bin/true\",), (\"/usr/bin/true\",)})\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _git(repo: str, *args: str, check: bool = True) -> str:\n27\t    proc = subprocess.run([\"git\", \"-C\", repo, *args], capture_output=True, text=True)\n28\t    if check and proc.returncode != 0:\n29\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"git {' '.join(args)} failed: {proc.stderr.strip()}\")\n30\t    return proc.stdout\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _slug(task: str) -> str:\n34\t    base = re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", task.lower()).strip(\"-\")[:28] or \"task\"\n35\t    return f\"{base}-{sha256_hex(task.encode())[:6]}\"   # unique per task, stable\n36\t\n37\t\n38\t@runtime_checkable\n39\tclass Coder(Protocol):\n40\t    def code(self, task: str, workdir: str) -> str: ...   # perform the change; return a summary\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tclass ClaudeCoder:\n44\t    \"\"\"Headless Claude Code (`claude -p`) run inside the repo — the real ARTIFICER worker.\"\"\"\n45\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\", timeout: int = 600):\n46\t        self.claude_bin, self.timeout = claude_bin, timeout\n47\t\n48\t    def code(self, task: str, workdir: str) -> str:\n49\t        prompt = (f\"You are working in the repo at {workdir}. Task: {task}\\n\"\n50\t                  \"Make the minimal change to accomplish it. Do not run git. Do not touch unrelated files.\")\n51\t        # acceptEdits lets the headless coder actually apply Edit/Write in its sandboxed repo (it\n52\t        # cannot prompt for permission in -p mode). ARTIFICER's isolation is the per-task branch;\n53\t        # git push / deploy stay A3 and are never invoked here.\n54\t        try:\n55\t            proc = subprocess.run(\n56\t                [self.claude_bin, \"-p\", prompt, \"--permission-mode\", \"acceptEdits\"],\n57\t                cwd=workdir, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n58\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n59\t            return f\"(coder error: {e})\"\n60\t        return (proc.stdout or \"\").strip()[:400]\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tclass Artificer(Agent):\n64\t    name = \"ARTIFICER\"\n65\t    mandate = \"own background coding tasks: tests before done, PRs not pushes\"\n66\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self, task: str, *, repo: str, test_cmd: Optional[List[str]] = None,\n69\t            coder: Optional[Coder] = None, branch: Optional[str] = None) -> AgentResult:\n70\t        coder = coder or ClaudeCoder()\n71\t        base = _git(repo, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\").strip()\n72\t        branch = branch or f\"artificer/{_slug(task)}\"\n73\t\n74\t        # ISOLATION: the coder works in a dedicated git WORKTREE off `base`, so the real working\n75\t        # tree is never touched, no pre-existing/unrelated edits get bundled, and a failure leaves\n76\t        # the main repo exactly as it was. The per-task branch survives for the human to review.\n77\t        _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"prune\", check=False)\n78\t        wt = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"artificer-wt-\")\n79\t        _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"add\", \"-B\", branch, wt, base)\n80\t        try:\n81\t            summary = coder.code(task, wt)\n82\t            changed = bool(_git(wt, \"status\", \"--porcelain\", check=False).strip())\n83\t            diff = _git(wt, \"diff\", \"--stat\", check=False).strip()\n84\t\n85\t            if not changed:\n86\t                res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n87\t                res.notes.append(\"no change produced for the task — nothing to propose.\")\n88\t                return res\n89\t\n90\t            real_test = test_cmd is not None and tuple(test_cmd) not in _TRIVIAL_TEST\n91\t            if not real_test:\n92\t                # NEVER claim \"tests passing\" without a real test command — record UNVERIFIED, withhold PR.\n93\t                res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"finding\", {\n94\t                    \"summary\": f\"ARTIFICER task '{task}': change made but NO real test command was run — UNVERIFIED, PR withheld\",\n95\t                    \"branch\": branch, \"diffstat\": diff, \"coder\": summary}, Tier.A1)])\n96\t                res.notes.append(\"no real test command → change is UNVERIFIED; PR withheld (correctness discipline)\")\n97\t                return res\n98\t\n99\t            test = subprocess.run(test_cmd, cwd=wt, capture_output=True, text=True)\n100\t            if test.returncode != 0:\n101\t                tail = (test.stdout + test.stderr).strip().splitlines()\n102\t                res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"finding\", {\n103\t                    \"summary\": f\"ARTIFICER task '{task}': change made but tests FAIL — PR withheld\",\n104\t                    \"branch\": branch, \"test_tail\": tail[-3:] if tail else [], \"diffstat\": diff}, Tier.A1)])\n105\t                res.notes.append(\"tests failed after the change — PR withheld (correctness discipline)\")\n106\t                return res\n107\t\n108\t            # tests genuinely pass → commit on the branch (in the worktree), propose a PR (A2, QUEUED).\n109\t            _git(wt, \"add\", \"-A\")\n110\t            _git(wt, \"commit\", \"-m\", f\"ARTIFICER: {task}\", check=False)\n111\t            res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"pr\", {\n112\t                \"subject\": task, \"branch\": branch, \"base\": base, \"diffstat\": diff,\n113\t                \"summary\": summary, \"tests\": f\"passing ({' '.join(test_cmd)})\",\n114\t                \"note\": \"ready-for-review; ARTIFICER does NOT push — a human reviews and pushes\"}, Tier.A2)])\n115\t            res.notes.append(f\"task done, tests PASS → PR proposed on branch '{branch}' (A2, awaiting approval)\")\n116\t            return res\n117\t        finally:\n118\t            _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"remove\", \"--force\", wt, check=False)\n119\t            shutil.rmtree(wt, ignore_errors=True)\n120\t"}]},"uuid":"d466fb49-f757-4d77-9798-4cb2044e4b33","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:50.792Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5d036404-45af-4548-9c59-1c786376d2cf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"d466fb49-f757-4d77-9798-4cb2044e4b33","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9t8Ut93DA3KW7ZWWFWs","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GdKWi7ir7GrpGnHsA7VUCC","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/bastion.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10552,"cache_read_input_tokens":18519,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10552,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9t8TqdnCKpyNQe497fg","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"7869f6eb-90d2-4f01-951c-48b073bfb14b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:51.040Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"7869f6eb-90d2-4f01-951c-48b073bfb14b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GdKWi7ir7GrpGnHsA7VUCC","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"BASTION (SIGIL §4.8) — defensive security posture over the owner's OWN infrastructure ONLY.\n2\tCeiling A1 (writes findings; remediation/patching is A3 and never taken here). Checks: TLS cert\n3\texpiry, dependency CVE exposure, and uptime — all OBSERVATIONAL. There is NO exploit, no port\n4\tsweep, no third-party target: BASTION iterates an allowlisted asset INVENTORY, and any target not\n5\tin that allowlist is REFUSED and logged (`refusal` record). This makes \"own systems only\" a\n6\tstructural property, not a promise (SIGIL §4.8 doctrine; enforced, not documented).\n7\t\n8\tGROUNDING (serve-the-quote, reused from SCHOLAR/consolidation): every finding carries the verbatim\n9\tobserved fact it stands on — the real cert `notAfter`, the exact manifest line, the probe status —\n10\tnever a model's guess. A dependency is flagged ONLY when its parsed version PROVABLY falls in an\n11\tadvisory's affected range; an unparseable version is a non-assessment, never a fabricated CVE.\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport ssl\n17\timport urllib.request\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n19\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n20\tfrom pathlib import Path\n21\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Protocol, Tuple, runtime_checkable\n22\t\n23\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n24\t\n25\t_PIN = re.compile(r\"^([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*)\\s*==\\s*([0-9][0-9A-Za-z.\\-]*)\")\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n29\tclass Asset:\n30\t    \"\"\"One allowlisted own-infra asset. `kind` ∈ {tls, deps, uptime}; `ref` is host:port / manifest\n31\t    path / url. Membership in the inventory IS the authorization to observe it.\"\"\"\n32\t    name: str\n33\t    kind: str\n34\t    ref: str\n35\t    meta: Optional[dict] = None\n36\t\n37\t\n38\t# --- version reasoning (strict, fail-closed to NON-assessment — never a fabricated vuln) ----------\n39\tdef _ver_tuple(v: Optional[str]) -> Optional[tuple]:\n40\t    \"\"\"A purely numeric dotted version → tuple; anything else (rc/beta/git/empty) → None (can't\n41\t    assess). Being strict here is what keeps CVE matching near-zero-false-positive.\"\"\"\n42\t    if not v:\n43\t        return None\n44\t    parts = str(v).strip().lstrip(\"vV\").split(\".\")\n45\t    out = []\n46\t    for p in parts:\n47\t        # ASCII decimals ONLY — str.isdigit() also accepts superscripts/other-script digits that\n48\t        # int() rejects or reinterprets, so guard on isascii() too (truly fail-closed, never a crash).\n49\t        if not (p.isascii() and p.isdigit()):\n50\t            return None\n51\t        out.append(int(p))\n52\t    return tuple(out) if out else None\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef _cmp(a: tuple, b: tuple) -> int:\n56\t    n = max(len(a), len(b))\n57\t    a = a + (0,) * (n - len(a))\n58\t    b = b + (0,) * (n - len(b))\n59\t    return (a > b) - (a < b)\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef _affected(version: str, introduced: Optional[str], fixed: Optional[str]) -> Optional[bool]:\n63\t    \"\"\"True iff introduced <= version < fixed. None if it cannot be proven either way (unparseable\n64\t    version/bound) — the caller treats None as 'no finding' (honest non-assessment).\"\"\"\n65\t    vt = _ver_tuple(version)\n66\t    if vt is None:\n67\t        return None\n68\t    it = _ver_tuple(introduced) if introduced else (0,)\n69\t    if it is None:\n70\t        return None\n71\t    if fixed:\n72\t        ft = _ver_tuple(fixed)\n73\t        if ft is None:\n74\t            return None\n75\t        if _cmp(vt, ft) >= 0:\n76\t            return False\n77\t    return _cmp(vt, it) >= 0\n78\t\n79\t\n80\t# --- capture seams (real by default, injectable doubles for offline tests) ------------------------\n81\t@runtime_checkable\n82\tclass CertSource(Protocol):\n83\t    def pem(self, ref: str) -> Optional[str]: ...    # ref=\"host:port\" → leaf cert PEM, or None\n84\t\n85\t\n86\t@runtime_checkable\n87\tclass UptimeSource(Protocol):\n88\t    def probe(self, ref: str) -> Tuple[bool, int]: ...   # (up, status_code)\n89\t\n90\t\n91\tclass SocketCertSource:\n92\t    \"\"\"Fetch the leaf TLS cert from an OWN host. Only ever called for allowlisted refs (BASTION\n93\t    guards before calling). Returns None on any error (no reachable cert = no finding).\"\"\"\n94\t    def __init__(self, timeout: int = 8):\n95\t        self.timeout = timeout\n96\t\n97\t    def pem(self, ref: str) -> Optional[str]:\n98\t        host, _, port = ref.partition(\":\")\n99\t        try:\n100\t            return ssl.get_server_certificate((host, int(port or 443)), timeout=self.timeout)\n101\t        except (OSError, ssl.SSLError, ValueError):\n102\t            return None\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n106\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx. A redirect target is chosen by the (possibly compromised) allowlisted\n107\t    server and is NOT itself allowlisted — following it would let BASTION's probe reach a host outside\n108\t    the own-infra scope, silently and un-audited (red-pen RP-BASTION-01). The 3xx is the status.\"\"\"\n109\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n110\t        return None\n111\t\n112\t\n113\tclass UrllibUptimeSource:\n114\t    def __init__(self, timeout: int = 8):\n115\t        self.timeout = timeout\n116\t        self._opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_NoRedirect)   # never auto-follows a redirect\n117\t\n118\t    def probe(self, ref: str) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n119\t        try:\n120\t            req = urllib.request.Request(ref, method=\"HEAD\",\n121\t                                         headers={\"User-Agent\": \"SIGIL-BASTION/1.0 (own-infra uptime)\"})\n122\t            with self._opener.open(req, timeout=self.timeout) as r:\n123\t                return (200 <= r.status < 500, r.status)\n124\t        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:       # a 5xx is 'down'; a 3xx (not followed) is 'up' + its code\n125\t            return (e.code < 500, e.code)\n126\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n127\t            return (False, 0)\n128\t\n129\t\n130\tclass Bastion(Agent):\n131\t    name = \"BASTION\"\n132\t    mandate = \"defensive posture over OWN infrastructure only; observe, never remediate\"\n133\t    ceiling = Tier.A1\n134\t\n135\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, inventory: Optional[List[Asset]] = None,\n136\t                 cve_feed: Optional[List[dict]] = None,\n137\t                 cert_source: Optional[CertSource] = None,\n138\t                 uptime_source: Optional[UptimeSource] = None):\n139\t        super().__init__(store)\n140\t        self.inventory = list(inventory or [])\n141\t        self._allow = {a.ref for a in self.inventory}          # THE own-infra allowlist\n142\t        self.cve_feed = list(cve_feed or [])\n143\t        self.cert_source = cert_source or SocketCertSource()\n144\t        self.uptime_source = uptime_source or UrllibUptimeSource()\n145\t\n146\t    # --- scope doctrine (structural own-infra-only) -----------------------------------------------\n147\t    def _refuse(self, ref: str, reason: str) -> int:\n148\t        return self.store.append(\n149\t            kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n150\t            payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": ref,\n151\t                     \"reason\": reason,\n152\t                     \"doctrine\": \"BASTION observes only the allowlisted own-infra inventory (SIGIL §4.8); \"\n153\t                                 \"no third-party scanning, no exploit tooling.\"})\n154\t\n155\t    def probe_target(self, ref: str, kind: str, *, now_iso: Optional[str] = None) -> AgentResult:\n156\t        \"\"\"Guarded ad-hoc entry. A ref outside the own-infra allowlist is REFUSED and logged — it is\n157\t        never scanned. This is the doctrine's structural teeth.\"\"\"\n158\t        if ref not in self._allow:\n159\t            self._refuse(ref, \"ad-hoc target is not in the own-infra allowlist\")\n160\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n161\t            res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {ref}: outside own-infra scope (logged, not scanned)\")\n162\t            return res\n163\t        return self._dispatch(self._assess(Asset(name=ref, kind=kind, ref=ref), now_iso=now_iso))\n164\t\n165\t    def _assess(self, asset: Asset, *, now_iso: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Proposal]:\n166\t        if asset.ref not in self._allow:            # defense in depth — never scan an unlisted ref\n167\t            self._refuse(asset.ref, \"asset not in the own-infra allowlist\")\n168\t            return []\n169\t        if asset.kind == \"tls\":\n170\t            return self._cert(asset, now_iso)\n171\t        if asset.kind == \"deps\":\n172\t            return self._deps(asset)\n173\t        if asset.kind == \"uptime\":\n174\t            return self._uptime(asset)\n175\t        return []\n176\t\n177\t    # --- scanners (observational; each finding carries its verbatim ground truth) -----------------\n178\t    def _cert(self, asset: Asset, now_iso: Optional[str]) -> List[Proposal]:\n179\t        pem = self.cert_source.pem(asset.ref)\n180\t        if not pem:\n181\t            return []\n182\t        try:\n183\t            from cryptography import x509\n184\t            cert = x509.load_pem_x509_certificate(pem.encode())\n185\t            not_after = getattr(cert, \"not_valid_after_utc\", None) or \\\n186\t                cert.not_valid_after.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n187\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unparseable cert is no finding, not a crash\n188\t            return []\n189\t        now = datetime.fromisoformat(now_iso) if now_iso else datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n190\t        if now.tzinfo is None:\n191\t            now = now.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n192\t        days = (not_after - now).days\n193\t        threshold = (asset.meta or {}).get(\"expiry_days\", 30)\n194\t        if days > threshold:\n195\t            return []\n196\t        sev = \"critical\" if days <= 0 else (\"high\" if days <= 7 else \"medium\")\n197\t        state = \"EXPIRED\" if days < 0 else f\"expires in {days}d\"\n198\t        return [Proposal(\"finding\", {\n199\t            \"check\": \"tls-cert-expiry\", \"asset\": asset.name, \"ref\": asset.ref, \"severity\": sev,\n200\t            \"days_to_expiry\": days,\n201\t            \"quote\": f\"notAfter={not_after.isoformat()}\",         # verbatim observed ground truth\n202\t            \"summary\": f\"TLS cert for {asset.name} {state} ({not_after.date()})\",\n203\t            \"assessed_at\": now.isoformat()}, Tier.A1)]\n204\t\n205\t    def _deps(self, asset: Asset) -> List[Proposal]:\n206\t        try:\n207\t            lines = Path(asset.ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\").splitlines()\n208\t        except OSError:\n209\t            return []\n210\t        props: List[Proposal] = []\n211\t        for raw in lines:\n212\t            m = _PIN.match(raw.strip())\n213\t            if not m:\n214\t                continue\n215\t            pkg, ver = m.group(1).lower(), m.group(2)\n216\t            for adv in self.cve_feed:\n217\t                if str(adv.get(\"package\", \"\")).lower() != pkg:\n218\t                    continue\n219\t                if _affected(ver, adv.get(\"introduced\"), adv.get(\"fixed\")) is not True:\n220\t                    continue                         # only a PROVEN match fires (None/False → skip)\n221\t                props.append(Proposal(\"finding\", {\n222\t                    \"check\": \"dependency-cve\", \"asset\": asset.name, \"ref\": asset.ref,\n223\t                    \"severity\": str(adv.get(\"severity\", \"medium\")), \"cve\": adv.get(\"id\"),\n224\t                    \"package\": pkg, \"version\": ver,\n225\t                    \"quote\": raw.strip(),            # verbatim manifest line — the ground truth\n226\t                    \"advisory\": f\"{adv.get('id')}: affected {adv.get('introduced') or '0'} \"\n227\t                                f\"..< {adv.get('fixed') or '∞'}\",\n228\t                    \"summary\": f\"{pkg} {ver} is affected by {adv.get('id')} \"\n229\t                               f\"({str(adv.get('summary', ''))[:60]})\",\n230\t                    \"fixed_in\": adv.get(\"fixed\")}, Tier.A1))\n231\t        return props\n232\t\n233\t    def _uptime(self, asset: Asset) -> List[Proposal]:\n234\t        up, status = self.uptime_source.probe(asset.ref)\n235\t        if up:\n236\t            return []\n237\t        return [Proposal(\"finding\", {\n238\t            \"check\": \"uptime\", \"asset\": asset.name, \"ref\": asset.ref, \"severity\": \"high\",\n239\t            \"quote\": f\"HEAD {asset.ref} → status={status}\",\n240\t            \"summary\": f\"{asset.name} appears DOWN (probe status {status})\"}, Tier.A1)]\n241\t\n242\t    @staticmethod\n243\t    def _key(payload: dict) -> tuple:\n244\t        \"\"\"A finding's IDENTITY: (asset, check, cve). The `cve` discriminator keeps N concurrent CVEs\n245\t        on one manifest distinct (RP-2) and lets a re-run resolve exactly the right prior finding.\"\"\"\n246\t        return (payload.get(\"asset\"), payload.get(\"check\"), payload.get(\"cve\"))\n247\t\n248\t    def _resolutions(self, fired_keys: set) -> List[Proposal]:\n249\t        \"\"\"For any (asset,check,cve) we flagged before that this run did NOT re-flag — and whose asset\n250\t        we actually re-assessed — emit a `resolved` supersession, so the brief stops presenting a fixed\n251\t        problem as current (RP-1/RP-5). A resolution supersedes the stale finding (latest wins).\"\"\"\n252\t        assessed = {a.name for a in self.inventory}\n253\t        latest: dict = {}\n254\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n255\t            if r.kind == \"finding\" and r.actor == self.name:\n256\t                latest[self._key(r.payload)] = r        # iter is seq-ascending → last seen = newest\n257\t        props: List[Proposal] = []\n258\t        for key, r in latest.items():\n259\t            if r.payload.get(\"resolved\"):\n260\t                continue                                # already resolved — don't churn\n261\t            asset_name, check, cve = key\n262\t            if asset_name in assessed and key not in fired_keys:\n263\t                props.append(Proposal(\"finding\", {\n264\t                    \"check\": check, \"asset\": asset_name, \"cve\": cve, \"resolved\": True,\n265\t                    \"severity\": \"info\", \"resolves_seq\": r.seq,\n266\t                    \"summary\": f\"{asset_name} {check}{(' ' + str(cve)) if cve else ''} resolved — re-assessed clean\",\n267\t                    \"quote\": \"resolved: prior finding no longer reproduces\"},\n268\t                    Tier.A1, supersedes_id=r.seq))\n269\t        return props\n270\t\n271\t    def run(self, *, now_iso: Optional[str] = None) -> AgentResult:\n272\t        proposals: List[Proposal] = []\n273\t        for asset in self.inventory:\n274\t            proposals.extend(self._assess(asset, now_iso=now_iso))\n275\t        fired = {self._key(p.payload) for p in proposals}\n276\t        resolutions = self._resolutions(fired)\n277\t        res = self._dispatch(proposals + resolutions)\n278\t        res.notes.append(f\"assessed {len(self.inventory)} own-infra asset(s) → {len(proposals)} finding(s)\"\n279\t                         + (f\", {len(resolutions)} resolved\" if resolutions else \"\"))\n280\t        return res\n281\t"}]},"uuid":"27d0d94a-725c-4616-9247-a1d171665acf","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:51.072Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7869f6eb-90d2-4f01-951c-48b073bfb14b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

An ExtractionProvider turns a\n2\twindow of spine records into candidate facts. Three implementations:\n3\t\n4\t  * AgentProvider   — headless `claude -p` on the Max plan (D5: agent-driven, zero API cost).\n5\t  * ReplayProvider  — replays a captured golden fixture (zero cost, exercises the parse path).\n6\t  * HeuristicProvider — offline keyword extraction (no LLM; a weak fallback + deterministic\n7\t                        double for tests).\n8\t\n9\tNone of these is trusted: whatever a provider proposes is only a CANDIDATE. The gate\n10\t(gate.admit) re-executes every citation before anything becomes a fact. The extraction prompt\n11\tstill injects the prove-don't-guess doctrine so the agent is asked to cite verbatim and refuse\n12\tto invent — belt (honest prompt) and suspenders (re-execution gate).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\timport json\n16\timport re\n17\timport subprocess\n18\tfrom pathlib import Path\n19\tfrom typing import Iterable, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..spine.models import SpineRecord\n22\tfrom .models import CandidateFact\n23\t\n24\t# the prove-don't-guess doctrine, distilled for extraction (framework/cognitive/metacognition.md).\n25\tDOCTRINE = (\n26\t    \"You extract DURABLE FACTS from a person's work log. A `decision` is a CHOICE the person \"\n27\t    \"COMMITTED TO ('we'll use X', 'decided to Y') — NOT a status update, test result, log line, \"\n28\t    \"or an instruction to someone else. A `commitment` is a PROMISE the person made, ideally with a \"\n29\t    \"due date. An `entity` is a durable named thing (project/person/tool/component). If a record is \"\n30\t    \"just narration, a result, or a task assignment, extract NOTHING from it. Rules, above all else:\\n\"\n31\t    \"1. PROVE, DON'T GUESS. Only report a fact that the provided records EXPLICITLY support. \"\n32\t    \"Never infer, embellish, or invent a decision, commitment, or entity that is not in the text.\\n\"\n33\t    \"2. CITE VERBATIM. For every fact, cite the exact record seq(s) and copy a `quote` that is a \"\n34\t    \"VERBATIM substring of a cited record. If you cannot quote it word-for-word, do not report it. \"\n35\t    \"The `statement` must be a COMPRESSION of your quote — use ONLY words that appear in the quote \"\n36\t    \"(you may drop and reorder words, but never add a word the quote does not contain).\\n\"\n37\t    \"3. REFUSE HONESTLY. If a record supports no durable fact, return fewer facts — an empty list \"\n38\t    \"is a correct answer. Do not pad.\\n\"\n39\t    \"4. DON'T FABRICATE CONFIDENCE. `confidence` is your rough estimate only; it never makes a \"\n40\t    \"weakly-supported claim strong.\\n\"\n41\t    \"5. CONTRADICTIONS. If the records show the person REVERSING an earlier decision (choosing X \"\n42\t    \"then later choosing not-X or a different option for the SAME subject), emit a kind='contradiction' \"\n43\t    \"whose source_seqs cite BOTH the earlier and later records AND provide a `quotes` array with a \"\n44\t    \"VERBATIM quote from EACH of those two records. Only flag genuine OPPOSITION, never a restatement \"\n45\t    \"or refinement of the same choice.\\n\"\n46\t)\n47\t\n48\t_SCHEMA_HINT = (\n49\t    \"Return ONLY a JSON array (no prose) of objects: \"\n50\t    '{\"kind\": \"decision\"|\"commitment\"|\"entity\"|\"contradiction\", \"subject\": \"<what it is about>\", '\n51\t    '\"statement\": \"<advisory summary in the person\\'s terms>\", \"quote\": \"<verbatim span from a cited record>\", '\n52\t    '\"quotes\": [\"<verbatim from record A>\", \"<verbatim from record B>\"] (contradictions ONLY, one per conflicting record), '\n53\t    '\"source_seqs\": [<int seq>...], \"owner\": \"<for commitments, who>\"|null, '\n54\t    '\"due_iso\": \"<ISO date if a due date is stated>\"|null, \"confidence\": <0..1>}.'\n55\t)\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t# a fast, cheap model is ideal for this mechanical extraction task.\n59\tFAST_MODEL = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\"\n60\t\n61\t\n62\t@runtime_checkable\n63\tclass ExtractionProvider(Protocol):\n64\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]: ...\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef render_window(records: Iterable[SpineRecord], *, max_chars: int = 1200) -> str:\n68\t    lines = []\n69\t    for r in records:\n70\t        txt = (r.text() or \"\").replace(\"\\n\", \" \").strip()\n71\t        if not txt:\n72\t            continue\n73\t        lines.append(f\"[seq {r.seq}] ({r.kind} · {r.actor}) {txt[:max_chars]}\")\n74\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n75\t\n76\t\n77\tdef build_prompt(records: list[SpineRecord]) -> str:\n78\t    \"\"\"The extraction prompt every LLM provider sends (doctrine + schema + rendered window).\"\"\"\n79\t    return f\"{DOCTRINE}\\n{_SCHEMA_HINT}\\n\\nRECORDS:\\n{render_window(records)}\\n\"\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef parse_candidates(raw: str, *, extractor: str) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n83\t    \"\"\"Robustly pull the JSON array out of a model response (tolerates ```json fences / prose).\"\"\"\n84\t    if not raw:\n85\t        return []\n86\t    fenced = re.search(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*(\\[.*?\\])\\s*```\", raw, re.S)\n87\t    body = fenced.group(1) if fenced else None\n88\t    if body is None:\n89\t        start, end = raw.find(\"[\"), raw.rfind(\"]\")\n90\t        body = raw[start:end + 1] if 0 <= start < end else None\n91\t    if body is None:\n92\t        return []\n93\t    try:\n94\t        items = json.loads(body)\n95\t    except json.JSONDecodeError:\n96\t        return []\n97\t    out = []\n98\t    for it in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n99\t        if not isinstance(it, dict) or it.get(\"kind\") not in (\"decision\", \"commitment\", \"entity\", \"contradiction\"):\n100\t            continue\n101\t        seqs = it.get(\"source_seqs\") or []\n102\t        seqs = [int(s) for s in seqs if isinstance(s, (int, float, str)) and str(s).lstrip(\"-\").isdigit()]\n103\t        raw_quotes = it.get(\"quotes\") or []\n104\t        quotes = tuple(str(q) for q in raw_quotes if isinstance(q, str) and q.strip()) if isinstance(raw_quotes, list) else ()\n105\t        out.append(CandidateFact(\n106\t            kind=it[\"kind\"], subject=str(it.get(\"subject\", \"\")).strip(),\n107\t            statement=str(it.get(\"statement\", \"\")).strip(), quote=str(it.get(\"quote\", \"\")),\n108\t            source_seqs=seqs, model_confidence=float(it.get(\"confidence\", 0.5) or 0.5),\n109\t            owner=(it.get(\"owner\") or None), due_iso=(it.get(\"due_iso\") or None),\n110\t            extractor=extractor, quotes=quotes))\n111\t    return out\n112\t\n113\t\n114\tclass AgentProvider:\n115\t    \"\"\"`--provider claude`: headless Claude Code on the MAX plan (D5, owner's preference).\n116\t    Spawns `claude -p --model <fast> <prompt>` per window — no metered API key needed. A fast\n117\t    model (Haiku) keeps each batch quick and cheap for this mechanical extraction.\"\"\"\n118\t\n119\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\",\n120\t                 model: str = FAST_MODEL, timeout: int = 180) -> None:\n121\t        self.claude_bin = claude_bin\n122\t        self.model = model\n123\t        self.timeout = timeout\n124\t\n125\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n126\t        if not records:\n127\t            return []\n128\t        cmd = [self.claude_bin, \"-p\", build_prompt(records)]\n129\t        if self.model:\n130\t            cmd += [\"--model\", self.model]\n131\t        try:\n132\t            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n133\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n134\t            return []\n135\t        return parse_candidates(proc.stdout, extractor=\"agent\")\n136\t\n137\t\n138\tclass ApiProvider:\n139\t    \"\"\"`--provider api`: the metered Anthropic Messages API (Claude API key). Reads the key\n140\t    from SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY / ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (or ~/.sigil/sigil.env via config). Raw\n141\t    HTTP — no SDK dependency. Use when you want the API rather than the Max subscription.\"\"\"\n142\t\n143\t    def __init__(self, model: str = FAST_MODEL, api_key: str | None = None,\n144\t                 timeout: int = 120, max_tokens: int = 2048) -> None:\n145\t        import os\n146\t        self.model = model\n147\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\")\n148\t        self.timeout = timeout\n149\t        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\n150\t\n151\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n152\t        if not records:\n153\t            return []\n154\t        if not self.api_key:\n155\t            raise RuntimeError(\"ApiProvider needs an API key — set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (or SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)\")\n156\t        import json as _json\n157\t        import urllib.request\n158\t        body = _json.dumps({\n159\t            \"model\": self.model, \"max_tokens\": self.max_tokens,\n160\t            \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": build_prompt(records)}],\n161\t        }).encode(\"utf-8\")\n162\t        req = urllib.request.Request(\n163\t            \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n164\t            headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\", \"x-api-key\": self.api_key,\n165\t                     \"anthropic-version\": \"2023-06-01\"})\n166\t        try:\n167\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n168\t                payload = _json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n169\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n170\t            return []\n171\t        text = \"\".join(b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in payload.get(\"content\", []) if isinstance(b, dict))\n172\t        return parse_candidates(text, extractor=\"api\")\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tclass LocalProvider:\n176\t    \"\"\"`--provider local`: a local Ollama model (fully offline, zero cost, no data leaves the\n177\t    box). Requires Ollama running at OLLAMA_HOST (default 127.0.0.1:11434) with `model` pulled.\"\"\"\n178\t\n179\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"llama3.1\", host: str | None = None, timeout: int = 180) -> None:\n180\t        import os\n181\t        self.model = model\n182\t        self.host = host or os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\") or \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"\n183\t        self.timeout = timeout\n184\t\n185\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n186\t        if not records:\n187\t            return []\n188\t        import json as _json\n189\t        import urllib.request\n190\t        body = _json.dumps({\"model\": self.model, \"prompt\": build_prompt(records), \"stream\": False}).encode(\"utf-8\")\n191\t        req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{self.host.rstrip('/')}/api/generate\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n192\t                                     headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\"})\n193\t        try:\n194\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n195\t                payload = _json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n196\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n197\t            return []\n198\t        return parse_candidates(payload.get(\"response\", \"\"), extractor=\"local\")\n199\t\n200\t\n201\tclass ReplayProvider:\n202\t    \"\"\"Replays a captured fixture (a JSON array of candidate dicts). Zero cost; proves the\n203\t    parse/gate path end-to-end without a live call.\"\"\"\n204\t\n205\t    def __init__(self, fixture: str | Path) -> None:\n206\t        self.fixture = Path(fixture)\n207\t\n208\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n209\t        return parse_candidates(self.fixture.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"), extractor=\"replay\")\n210\t\n211\t\n212\t_DECISION_CUES = (\"decided to\", \"we'll go with\", \"going with\", \"chose to\", \"chosen:\", \"decision:\")\n213\t_COMMIT_CUES = (\"i'll \", \"i will \", \"we'll \", \"commit to\", \"todo:\", \"next step\", \"by end of\", \"due \")\n214\t\n215\t\n216\tclass HeuristicProvider:\n217\t    \"\"\"Offline, no-LLM keyword extractor — a weak fallback and a deterministic test double.\n218\t    Emits a candidate per cue-bearing sentence, quoting that sentence verbatim (so the gate\n219\t    grounds it). Deliberately conservative; the AgentProvider is the real extractor.\"\"\"\n220\t\n221\t    def extract(self, records: list[SpineRecord]) -> list[CandidateFact]:\n222\t        out = []\n223\t        for r in records:\n224\t            for sent in re.split(r\"(?<=[.!?])\\s+\", (r.text() or \"\")):\n225\t                low = sent.lower().strip()\n226\t                if not low:\n227\t                    continue\n228\t                if any(c in low for c in _DECISION_CUES):\n229\t                    out.append(CandidateFact(\"decision\", low[:60], sent.strip(), sent.strip(),\n230\t                                             [r.seq], 0.5, extractor=\"heuristic\"))\n231\t                elif any(c in low for c in _COMMIT_CUES):\n232\t                    out.append(CandidateFact(\"commitment\", low[:60], sent.strip(), sent.strip(),\n233\t                                             [r.seq], 0.5, owner=\"owner\", extractor=\"heuristic\"))\n234\t        return out\n235\t"}]},"uuid":"0740ee34-b6bd-4db7-848b-99fcc38b9f1b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:51.770Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1fb61e0c-8526-4341-927b-755c7c06a4ba","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6c03054c-d5f0-4f39-9eee-cbc9996d9025","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9t96RZFQRffTs7tTQ2Y","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QuXVwdw3pFjEoMYuN46K7v","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/models.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":14089,"cache_read_input_tokens":29071,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":14089,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9t94Nn3xpmXwmiFUhri","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"979647ad-ea86-48ec-a285-ad24b0916837","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:57.487Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"979647ad-ea86-48ec-a285-ad24b0916837","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QuXVwdw3pFjEoMYuN46K7v","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The episodic spine record — one immutable event, source of truth (SIGIL §6.1).\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n5\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n6\tfrom typing import Any\n7\t\n8\t# event kinds (superset of the CRUCIBLE blackboard EventKind, extended for SIGIL sources)\n9\tKINDS = frozenset({\n10\t    \"message\", \"tool_call\", \"tool_result\", \"decision\", \"commitment\",\n11\t    \"commit\", \"document\", \"brief\", \"refusal\", \"email\", \"session\",\n12\t    \"entity\", \"contradiction\",  # promoted by the ARCHIVIST consolidation pass (§6.3)\n13\t    \"warden_checkpoint\",        # WARDEN (Phase 1) cross-anchors its action-log head here (anti-rollback)\n14\t    \"event\", \"finding\", \"interaction\", \"draft\",  # the agent mesh (Phase 3, §4): SENTINEL/BASTION/ENVOY\n15\t    \"report\", \"pr\",             # Phase 4: SCHOLAR sourced research reports, ARTIFICER PR proposals\n16\t})\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef now_iso() -> str:\n20\t    \"\"\"Informational wallclock timestamp. NOT part of the content digest (replay-stable).\"\"\"\n21\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n22\t\n23\t\n24\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n25\tclass SpineRecord:\n26\t    \"\"\"One line of the spine. `cert_digest`/`prev_hash`/`entry_hash` are the chain fields;\n27\t    everything above `ts` is the digested content (ts is informational, excluded from the digest).\"\"\"\n28\t    seq: int\n29\t    scope: str\n30\t    kind: str\n31\t    source: str            # \"claude-code\" | \"git\" | \"doc\" | ...\n32\t    actor: str             # \"user\" | \"assistant\" | session id | commit author | ...\n33\t    payload: dict[str, Any]\n34\t    parent_id: int | None\n35\t    supersedes_id: int | None\n36\t    ts: str\n37\t    cert_digest: str\n38\t    prev_hash: str\n39\t    entry_hash: str\n40\t\n41\t    @classmethod\n42\t    def from_dict(cls, d: dict[str, Any]) -> \"SpineRecord\":\n43\t        return cls(\n44\t            seq=d[\"seq\"], scope=d[\"scope\"], kind=d[\"kind\"], source=d[\"source\"],\n45\t            actor=d[\"actor\"], payload=d.get(\"payload\") or {},\n46\t            parent_id=d.get(\"parent_id\"), supersedes_id=d.get(\"supersedes_id\"),\n47\t            ts=d.get(\"ts\", \"\"), cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"],\n48\t            prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"],\n49\t        )\n50\t\n51\t    def text(self) -> str:\n52\t        \"\"\"Best-effort human/searchable text of the record (for embedding + display).\"\"\"\n53\t        p = self.payload or {}\n54\t        for key in (\"text\", \"content\", \"message\", \"summary\", \"body\", \"title\"):\n55\t            v = p.get(key)\n56\t            if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():\n57\t                return v\n58\t        # tool records / structured payloads: compact JSON fallback\n59\t        import json\n60\t        return json.dumps(p, ensure_ascii=False)[:4000]\n61\t"}]},"uuid":"ec1720ab-dff8-44eb-a6a5-a7378d3164a2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:57.500Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"979647ad-ea86-48ec-a285-ad24b0916837","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ec1720ab-dff8-44eb-a6a5-a7378d3164a2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9t96RZFQRffTs7tTQ2Y","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01KjeT27DjQiYKzYbPy9t4k2","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/store.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":14089,"cache_read_input_tokens":29071,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":14089,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9t94Nn3xpmXwmiFUhri","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"38b3d2a1-6009-421a-9664-cd7393bc5bc6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:57.634Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"38b3d2a1-6009-421a-9664-cd7393bc5bc6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KjeT27DjQiYKzYbPy9t4k2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n17\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n18\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n22\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n23\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n24\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n25\t        end = f.tell()\n26\t        if end == 0:\n27\t            return None\n28\t        buf = b\"\"\n29\t        pos = end\n30\t        while pos > 0:\n31\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n32\t            pos -= step\n33\t            f.seek(pos)\n34\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n35\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n36\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n37\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n38\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n39\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass SpineStore:\n43\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n44\t        self.path = Path(path)\n45\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n47\t\n48\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\t    def append(\n50\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n51\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n52\t        ts: str | None = None,\n53\t    ) -> int:\n54\t        content = {\n55\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n56\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n57\t        }\n58\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n59\t        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n60\t        record = {\n61\t            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n62\t            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n63\t        }\n64\t        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n65\t            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n66\t        self._last = entry\n67\t        return entry.seq\n68\t\n69\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n71\t        if not self.path.exists():\n72\t            return\n73\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n74\t            for line in f:\n75\t                line = line.strip()\n76\t                if not line:\n77\t                    continue\n78\t                d = json.loads(line)\n79\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n80\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n81\t\n82\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n83\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n84\t            if r.seq == seq:\n85\t                return r\n86\t            if r.seq > seq:\n87\t                break\n88\t        return None\n89\t\n90\t    @property\n91\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n92\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n93\t\n94\t    def count(self) -> int:\n95\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n96\t\n97\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n98\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n99\t        return [\n100\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n101\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n102\t        ]\n103\t\n104\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n105\t        \"\"\"Two-layer integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n106\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link\n107\t        cleanly (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). A payload edit fails (1); editing\n108\t        the digest to match cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash break caught by (2).\"\"\"\n109\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n110\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n111\t            content = {\n112\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n113\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n114\t            }\n115\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n116\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n117\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n118\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n119\t\n120\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n121\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n122\t        if not line:\n123\t            return None\n124\t        d = json.loads(line)\n125\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n126\t"}]},"uuid":"bf33b454-514b-4254-ad0d-10a5fd84aea5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:57.653Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"38b3d2a1-6009-421a-9664-cd7393bc5bc6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d7255a10-feec-4190-82e8-0427290dddc0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015wRGA2ozKK9W6vY1fPypNV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Voice → KERNEL dispatch. Recognized text is routed through the Phase-1 KERNEL (`sigil-kernel\n2\task`), so a voice request crosses the SAME T0 router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as any\n3\tother — voice is just another interface onto the one authorized path. The spoken response is the\n4\tKERNEL's answer body (the bracketed [T0…]/[WARDEN…] status lines are stripped).\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\timport subprocess\n8\tfrom pathlib import Path\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _default_bin() -> str:\n12\t    for p in (\"kernel/target/release/sigil-kernel\", \"kernel/target/debug/sigil-kernel\"):\n13\t        f = Path(\"/home/kali/sigil\") / p\n14\t        if f.exists():\n15\t            return str(f)\n16\t    return \"sigil-kernel\"\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tclass KernelDispatch:\n20\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 60):\n21\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n22\t        self.timeout = timeout\n23\t\n24\t    # exact KERNEL status-line prefixes to strip (so a legitimate answer line that merely starts\n25\t    # with '[' is NOT dropped, and a BLOCKED/error is NOT masked as an answer).\n26\t    _STATUS = (\"[t0\", \"[warden\", \"[blocked\", \"[direct\", \"[dispatch\")\n27\t\n28\t    def send(self, text: str) -> str:\n29\t        text = (text or \"\").strip()\n30\t        if not text:\n31\t            return \"I didn't catch that.\"\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"ask\", text],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n36\t            return f\"the kernel is unavailable ({e})\"\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            tail = (proc.stderr or proc.stdout).strip().splitlines()\n39\t            return f\"that request was refused or errored: {tail[-1][:200]}\" if tail else \"that request was refused.\"\n40\t        if any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(\"[blocked\") for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()):\n41\t            return \"that action needs your approval — it wasn't run.\"\n42\t        body = [ln.strip() for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()\n43\t                if ln.strip() and not any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(s) for s in self._STATUS)]\n44\t        answer = \" \".join(body).strip()\n45\t        return answer[:600] if answer else \"there's no answer for that in memory yet.\"\n46\t"}]},"uuid":"8ee8871b-45b4-47b1-9cda-0c17649310cf","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:01:58.325Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d7255a10-feec-4190-82e8-0427290dddc0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c75a2f1e-989d-4487-898b-f7dabb14f888","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012wBvSXYypcCwjE59g8oEoV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid OWNER signature over its (target, decision, approver).\n35\t    Unsigned, wrong-key, or no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n36\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n37\t    sig = p.get(\"sig\")\n38\t    if not sig or not trusted_pubkey_b64 or p.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey_b64:\n39\t        return False\n40\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n41\t    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey_b64, msg, sig)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None) -> List:\n45\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid owner approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a\n46\t    VERIFIED approval; an unsigned/forged/wrong-key approval leaves the item pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n47\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n48\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n49\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n50\t    resolved = set()\n51\t    queued = {}\n52\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n53\t        p = r.payload\n54\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64):\n55\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n56\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n57\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n58\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n62\t    pass\n63\t\n64\t\n65\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n66\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n67\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n68\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n69\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n70\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n71\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n72\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n73\t\n74\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n75\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n76\t            if r.seq == seq:\n77\t                return r\n78\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n79\t\n80\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n81\t        target = self._target(seq)\n82\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n83\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n84\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n85\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n86\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n87\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n88\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n89\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n90\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n91\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n92\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n93\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"13de0eb0-5fb6-4715-a0ea-0489a3c95714","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:07.361Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c75a2f1e-989d-4487-898b-f7dabb14f888","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"19842263-0ad2-447a-8701-8c5ce79eb2bd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KB5kPMsMTWvc8x9jajhiDU","type":"tool_result","content":"===find reuse & assert_no_offense===\nsigil/consolidate/__init__.py:6:zero framework.* modules (assert_no_offense enforced at import).\"\"\"\nsigil/consolidate/__init__.py:7:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/consolidate/__init__.py:9:assert_no_offense()  # doctrine §12: no engine module may be loaded in a SIGIL process\nsigil/mcp/server.py:16:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/mcp/server.py:21:assert_no_offense()  # doctrine §12: no engine module may be loaded in a SIGIL process\nsigil/reuse/__init__.py:6:`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\nsigil/reuse/__init__.py:22:    \"assert_no_offense\",\nsigil/reuse/__init__.py:26:def assert_no_offense() -> None:\nsigil/perception/__init__.py:9:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/perception/__init__.py:11:assert_no_offense()\n---reuse module---\nsigil/reuse\n===governor package===\nauthn.py\nbudget.py\ncore.py\nidentity.py\n__init__.py\nkillswitch.py\npromotion.py\n===main.rs===\n//! SIGIL KERNEL — the palette (Phase 1). A command interface to the KERNEL: route an intent\n//! through the cognition cascade (T0 → answer/escalate/dispatch), gate every tool invocation\n//! through WARDEN (tier A0–A3, fail-closed), and append a signed, chained action-log record. The\n//! self-audit replays that log verbatim (\"what did you do and why\", C18). A global hotkey +\n//! voice daemon are Phase 1.5/2; this CLI meets the acceptance bar: a full task round-trip,\n//! logged & tiered.\n\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\nuse std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n\nuse clap::{Parser, Subcommand};\nuse sigil_kernel::crypto::sha256_hex;\nuse sigil_kernel::router::{route, Route};\nuse sigil_kernel::tiers::Tier;\nuse sigil_kernel::warden::{Verdict, Warden};\nuse sigil_kernel::{anchor, cascade, memory};\n\n#[derive(Parser)]\n#[command(name = \"sigil-kernel\", about = \"SIGIL KERNEL — routed, gated, logged intent\")]\nstruct Cli {\n    #[command(subcommand)]\n    cmd: Cmd,\n}\n\n#[derive(Subcommand)]\nenum Cmd {\n    /// Route an intent through the cascade (T0 classify → dispatch), gated + logged by WARDEN.\n    Ask {\n        #[arg(required = true, trailing_var_arg = true)]\n        intent: Vec<String>,\n    },\n    /// Invoke a named tool directly through WARDEN — demonstrates tier classification + gating.\n    Do {\n        tool: String,\n        #[arg(trailing_var_arg = true)]\n        args: Vec<String>,\n    },\n    /// Replay the signed action log verbatim (self-audit, C18).\n    Audit,\n    /// Verify the action log's chain + signatures + the spine-anchored anti-rollback high-water.\n    /// FAILS CLOSED if the anchor is unreachable/unestablished; pass --allow-unanchored for a\n    /// genuine first run / offline check (weaker: local integrity only).\n    Verify {\n        #[arg(long)]\n        allow_unanchored: bool,\n    },\n    /// Cross-anchor the current action-log head into the append-only Phase-0 spine (anti-rollback).\n    Checkpoint,\n    /// Print the WARDEN public key.\n    Pubkey,\n}\n\nfn warden_dir() -> PathBuf {\n    let home = std::env::var(\"SIGIL_HOME\")\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!(\"{}/.sigil\", std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \".\".into())));\n    PathBuf::from(home).join(\"warden\")\n}\n\nfn now() -> u64 {\n    SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(0)\n}\n\nfn args_hash(s: &str) -> String {\n    format!(\"b3:{}\", &sha256_hex(s.as_bytes())[..12])\n}\n\nfn main() {\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    let w = Warden::open(&warden_dir()).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n        eprintln!(\"cannot open WARDEN: {e}\");\n        std::process::exit(1);\n    });\n\n    match cli.cmd {\n        Cmd::Ask { intent } => cmd_ask(&w, &intent.join(\" \")),\n        Cmd::Do { tool, args } => cmd_do(&w, &tool, &args.join(\" \")),\n        Cmd::Audit => cmd_audit(&w),\n        Cmd::Verify { allow_unanchored } => cmd_verify(&w, allow_unanchored),\n        Cmd::Checkpoint => cmd_checkpoint(&w),\n        Cmd::Pubkey => println!(\"{}\", w.public_hex()),\n    }\n}\n\nfn anchor_set(w: &Warden, count: u64, head_hash: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n    let pubkey = w.public_hex();\n    let sig = w.sign_hex(anchor::anchor_msg(count, head_hash, &pubkey).as_bytes());\n    anchor::set(count, head_hash, &pubkey, &sig)\n}\n\n/// Anchor the current head into the spine — best-effort, never fatal to the hot path.\nfn anchor_head(w: &Warden) {\n    if let Ok(Some((count, head_hash))) = w.head() {\n        if count > 0 {\n            let _ = anchor_set(w, count, &head_hash);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn cmd_checkpoint(w: &Warden) {\n    match w.head() {\n        Ok(Some((count, head_hash))) => match anchor_set(w, count, &head_hash) {\n            Ok(()) => println!(\"anchored action-log head into the spine: count={count} head={}\", &head_hash[..16.min(head_hash.len())]),\n            Err(e) => eprintln!(\"anchor failed: {e}\"),\n        },\n        Ok(None) => println!(\"nothing to anchor (action log empty)\"),\n        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"cannot read head: {e}\"),\n    }\n}\n\nfn cmd_ask(w: &Warden, intent: &str) {\n    let r = route(intent);\n    // Route → the tool the KERNEL will invoke. Cognition (answer/plan) is A0-class (no external\n    // effect); a real agent dispatch is recorded as an A1 intent note (the mesh is Phase 3).\n    let tool = match r {\n        Route::AnswerLocal => \"memory.search\",\n        Route::EscalateT1 => \"cascade.answer.t1\",\n        Route::EscalateT2 => \"cascade.answer.t2\",\n        Route::DispatchAgent => \"agent.dispatch.note\",\n    };\n    let v = w.decide(tool);\n    println!(\"[T0 {} → {}]  WARDEN: {} {}\", r.as_str(), tool, v.tier, v.decision.as_str());\n\n    if !v.decision.may_run() {\n        block(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, intent, &v);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    let output = match r {\n        Route::AnswerLocal => memory::search(intent, 5).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(memory unavailable: {e})\")),\n        Route::EscalateT1 => cascade::run(intent, cascade::FAST).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(T1 unavailable: {e})\")),\n        Route::EscalateT2 => cascade::run(intent, cascade::DEEP).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(T2 unavailable: {e})\")),\n        Route::DispatchAgent => format!(\n            \"[dispatch] the agent mesh (ARTIFICER/SCHOLAR/ENVOY) is Phase 3; the KERNEL recorded this intent:\\n  {intent}\"\n        ),\n    };\n    finish(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, intent, &v, &output);\n}\n\nfn cmd_do(w: &Warden, tool: &str, args: &str) {\n    let v = w.decide(tool);\n    println!(\"[direct tool {}]  WARDEN: {} {}\", tool, v.tier, v.decision.as_str());\n    if !v.decision.may_run() {\n        block(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, args, &v);\n        return;\n    }\n    // v1 has no real tool executors (those are the Phase-3 agents) — record an executed stub so\n    // the round-trip + tiering + signed log are demonstrable end-to-end.\n    let output = format!(\"[executed] {tool} {args}\").trim_end().to_string();\n    finish(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, args, &v, &output);\n}\n\nfn finish(w: &Warden, agent: &str, tool: &str, args: &str, v: &Verdict, output: &str) {\n    let rh = format!(\"b3:{}\", &sha256_hex(output.as_bytes())[..12]);\n    match w.record(agent, tool, &args_hash(args), v, \"auto\", &rh, now()) {\n        Ok(rec) => {\n            println!(\"\\n{output}\\n\");\n            println!(\n                \"[WARDEN {} {} — logged @ action spine seq {} · {}]\",\n                v.tier, v.decision.as_str(), rec.seq, &rec.entry_hash[..16]\n            );\n        }\n        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"action-log write failed: {e}\"),\n    }\n}\n\nfn block(w: &Warden, agent: &str, tool: &str, args: &str, v: &Verdict) {\n    let need = if v.tier == Tier::A3 { \"explicit per-action approval (no promotion)\" } else { \"one-tap approval\" };\n    let _ = w.record(agent, tool, &args_hash(args), v, \"none\", \"blocked:awaiting-approval\", now());\n    println!(\"[BLOCKED — {} requires {}; not executed. Logged as awaiting-approval.]\", v.tier, need);\n    anchor_head(w); // protect the evidence that a dangerous action was attempted (anti-rollback)\n}\n\nfn cmd_audit(w: &Warden) {\n    match w.records() {\n        Ok(recs) if recs.is_empty() => println!(\"(action log empty — no actions yet)\"),\n        Ok(recs) => {\n            println!(\"SIGIL action spine — {} record(s), verbatim (C18 self-audit):\\n\", recs.len());\n            for r in recs {\n                println!(\n                    \"  seq {:<3} {:<4} {:<20} {:<10} agent={:<10} approver={:<12} ts={}\",\n                    r.seq, r.tier, r.tool, r.decision, r.agent, r.approver, r.ts\n                );\n            }\n        }\n        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"audit failed: {e}\"),\n    }\n}\n\nfn cmd_verify(w: &Warden, allow_unanchored: bool) {\n    let n = match w.verify() {\n        Ok(n) => n,\n        Err(e) => {\n            eprintln!(\"action log FAIL: {e}\");\n            std::process::exit(2);\n        }\n    };\n    let on_disk = w.head().ok().flatten().map(|(c, _)| c).unwrap_or(0);\n\n    // Cross-check the spine-anchored monotonic high-water. The anchor read itself FAILS CLOSED:\n    // a bridge error, a tampered spine (the bridge exits non-zero), OR an established-but-now-zero\n    // high-water are all treated as UNVERIFIABLE — because the checker (bridge + spine) is under\n    // the same file-write attacker the anchor defends against, resolving its failure as \"OK\" would\n    // hand the attacker a clean bill. `--allow-unanchored` is the explicit escape for a genuine\n    // first run / offline check.\n    match anchor::high_water(&w.public_hex()) {\n        Ok((hw, _)) if on_disk < hw => {\n            eprintln!(\"action log FAIL: ROLLBACK — on-disk head count {on_disk} < spine-anchored high-water {hw} (recent records erased)\");\n            std::process::exit(2);\n        }\n        Ok((hw, _)) if hw > 0 => {\n            println!(\"action log OK: {n} record(s) verify — chain + signatures + anti-rollback anchor (spine high-water {hw})\");\n        }\n        // hw == 0 (never anchored for this key) or Err (bridge/spine unavailable) → unverifiable\n        other => {\n            if allow_unanchored {\n                let why = if other.is_ok() { \"no anchor established yet\" } else { \"anchor bridge unavailable\" };\n                println!(\"action log OK (LOCAL ONLY): {n} record(s) verify — chain + signatures; anti-rollback UNVERIFIED ({why}, --allow-unanchored)\");\n            } else if n == 0 {\n                println!(\"action log OK: empty log, nothing to anchor\");\n            } else {\n                eprintln!(\"action log UNVERIFIABLE: {n} record(s) pass local checks but the anti-rollback anchor is unreachable/unestablished — cannot certify freshness (a rollback+bridge-sabotage looks identical). Re-run with --allow-unanchored only if this is a genuine first run/offline.\");\n                std::process::exit(3);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"060426d2-1adb-41b0-9998-77d53379375f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:23.568Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"19842263-0ad2-447a-8701-8c5ce79eb2bd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"79d7daf0-bc8e-42e2-9ba6-26d5d342dc6e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FzjnrxUmR5WyJ2rNLDFsr3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL's vendored integrity substrate (owner's own CRUCIBLE work, copied verbatim).\n2\t\n3\tThe one seam that would ever couple to the engine — now fully self-contained. SIGIL\n4\timports NO `framework.*` module at all, which is the strongest form of the sovereignty\n5\tdoctrine (§1.3 / §12): the offensive engine cannot even be loaded into a SIGIL process.\n6\t`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport sys\n11\t\n12\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, digest_payload, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n13\tfrom .chain import append_entry, build_chain, sign_head, verify_chain, verify_head\n14\tfrom .crypto import generate_keypair, sign, verify_one, verify_threshold, KeyPair\n15\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n16\t\n17\t__all__ = [\n18\t    \"canonical_json\", \"digest_payload\", \"evidence_signing_bytes\", \"sha256_hex\",\n19\t    \"append_entry\", \"build_chain\", \"sign_head\", \"verify_chain\", \"verify_head\",\n20\t    \"generate_keypair\", \"sign\", \"verify_one\", \"verify_threshold\", \"KeyPair\",\n21\t    \"AuthorizerKey\", \"ChainEntry\", \"Signature\", \"SignedChainHead\", \"TrustRoot\",\n22\t    \"assert_no_offense\",\n23\t]\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef assert_no_offense() -> None:\n27\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed: NO framework.* module may be loaded in a SIGIL process (doctrine §12).\"\"\"\n28\t    leaked = [m for m in sys.modules if m == \"framework\" or m.startswith(\"framework.\")]\n29\t    if leaked:\n30\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"SIGIL sovereignty violation: engine modules loaded: {leaked}\")\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"5cff8f40-85cf-4491-919f-56478eb0c375","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:31.437Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"79d7daf0-bc8e-42e2-9ba6-26d5d342dc6e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
