{"parentUuid":"bba215cd-b32a-4a8f-b891-d05c66a969a4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FvPfQkSrj3fu5hsFomtiBc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The Governor (SIGIL §5) — the single decision point the agent dispatch gate consults for EVERY\n2\tproposal. It composes the kill switch, budgets, and promotion policy into one fail-closed verdict:\n3\tAUTO (write it), QUEUE (hold for human approval), or DENY (refuse + log). The order matters and is\n4\tdeliberately conservative: observe is always allowed; a kill halts everything above observe; budget\n5\tis checked before any auto-grant; A2 auto-approves only under an explicit promotion; A3 never autos.\n6\t\n7\tKill/promotion are read FRESH per decision (a mid-run kill halts the rest of the run). Backward\n8\tcompatible: with no kill, no cap, and no promotion, A0/A1≤ceiling → AUTO and A2/A3 → QUEUE, exactly\n9\tas the pre-Phase-6 gate behaved.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n14\tfrom enum import Enum\n15\tfrom typing import Optional\n16\t\n17\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger\n18\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch\n19\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass Outcome(Enum):\n23\t    AUTO = \"auto\"\n24\t    QUEUE = \"queue\"\n25\t    DENY = \"deny\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n29\tclass Decision:\n30\t    outcome: Outcome\n31\t    reason: str\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef _utc_day() -> str:\n35\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()   # runtime bucketing only (never digested)\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef _load_caps() -> BudgetCaps:\n39\t    \"\"\"Optional per-agent caps from ~/.sigil/budgets.json ({daily_actions, daily_interrupts}).\n40\t    Absent/malformed → uncapped (budgets opt-in; default behavior unchanged).\"\"\"\n41\t    try:\n42\t        import json\n43\t\n44\t        from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n45\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"budgets.json\"\n46\t        if not f.exists():\n47\t            return BudgetCaps()\n48\t        d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n49\t        return BudgetCaps(daily_actions=d.get(\"daily_actions\"), daily_interrupts=d.get(\"daily_interrupts\"))\n50\t    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\n51\t        return BudgetCaps()\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tclass Governor:\n55\t    def __init__(self, store, *, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None, day_iso: Optional[str] = None,\n56\t                 owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n57\t        from .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n58\t        self.store = store\n59\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n60\t        tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n61\t        self.kill = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n62\t        self.promo = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n63\t        self.budget = BudgetLedger(store, caps if caps is not None else _load_caps())\n64\t        self._day = day_iso\n65\t\n66\t    def decide(self, *, agent: str, tier, ceiling, scope: Optional[str] = None) -> Decision:\n67\t        from ..agents.base import AUTO_BAR, Tier\n68\t        scope = scope or \"*\"\n69\t\n70\t        # A0 observe/read is ALWAYS allowed — even under a kill switch (perception + memory stay alive).\n71\t        if tier <= Tier.A0:\n72\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, \"observe (A0)\")\n73\t\n74\t        # KILL SWITCH: the mesh is halted; nothing above observe may act.\n75\t        if self.kill.is_engaged():\n76\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, \"kill switch engaged — agent mesh halted (A0 observe only)\")\n77\t\n78\t        # BUDGET: fail-closed — at cap, deny.\n79\t        over, why = self.budget.over_budget(agent, self._day or _utc_day())\n80\t        if over:\n81\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, why)\n82\t\n83\t        # Within the auto bar (A0/A1) and the agent's ceiling → auto, as always.\n84\t        if tier <= AUTO_BAR and tier <= ceiling:\n85\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"within auto bar ({tier.label()})\")\n86\t\n87\t        # A2 may auto-approve ONLY under an explicit per-scope promotion (never for ENVOY).\n88\t        if tier == Tier.A2 and tier <= ceiling and self.promo.is_promoted(agent, scope):\n89\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"A2 auto-approved by promotion ({agent}/{scope})\")\n90\t\n91\t        # Everything else (A2 unpromoted, A3, or above ceiling) queues for explicit human approval.\n92\t        return Decision(Outcome.QUEUE, f\"queued for approval ({tier.label()})\")\n93\t"}]},"uuid":"2779f109-817b-4535-b28c-ac1dbf32b7fb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:31.947Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bba215cd-b32a-4a8f-b891-d05c66a969a4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"5249eb95-6f6e-4c4a-9d79-0b8a83f5e73c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01StUT2WvDxefgy1VnwmfUoX","type":"tool_result","content":"===warden.rs decide/record===\n20:pub struct Verdict {\n28:    pub fn open(dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Self> {\n37:    pub fn decide(&self, tool: &str) -> Verdict {\n39:        Verdict { tier, decision: gate(tier) }\n44:    pub fn record(\n49:        verdict: &Verdict,\n61:    pub fn verify(&self) -> Result<usize, String> {\n65:    pub fn records(&self) -> io::Result<Vec<ActionRecord>> {\n70:    pub fn head(&self) -> io::Result<Option<(u64, String)>> {\n74:    pub fn public_hex(&self) -> String {\n79:    pub fn sign_hex(&self, msg: &[u8]) -> String {\n107:    fn recorded_actions_verify_and_survive_reopen() {\n===cli.py perceive (~180-230)===\n185:    elif a.action == \"perceive\":\n187:        from .perception import Frame, Perceptor, grab_camera, grab_screen\n192:            frame = grab_camera()\n194:            frame = grab_screen()\n199:        res = Perceptor(store).perceive(a.question or \"\", frame)\n389:                                       \"artifice\", \"bastion\", \"perceive\"])","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"0f4f8e4b-fae3-458a-9e47-b1a6c4bf7694","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:37.846Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5249eb95-6f6e-4c4a-9d79-0b8a83f5e73c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

noqa: BLE001"}]},"uuid":"966514f3-90bb-41bb-8838-66dbaaaaaf09","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:44.031Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c2c3b39f-fbc1-4ba8-a5ad-d9962b01f07c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2d951e37-b3f9-4958-969c-8e5d52a0ce18","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Fwd94dbBRcoitmgMpHaCsw","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Grounding taxonomy + domain-neutral Beta belief math (SIGIL §6.3, ported from\n2\tworldmodel/models.py). Grounding is queryable METADATA that never moves belief; belief is a\n3\tBeta(alpha,beta) whose mean/LCB only ever drop on refutation (demote-only).\"\"\"\n4\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n5\t\n6\timport math\n7\t\n8\t# the source stamped on every consolidation-written record (distinguishes it from ingest).\n9\tCONSOLIDATE_SOURCE = \"archivist\"\n10\t\n11\t# grounding tags. A record is a FACT only if its grounding starts with GROUNDED_PREFIX.\n12\tGROUNDED_PREFIX = \"ingest:\"          # traces to real spine records that re-verified\n13\tUNGROUNDED = \"llm:ungrounded\"        # the model asserted it but re-execution failed → commentary\n14\t\n15\t\n16\tdef ground_tag(min_seq: int) -> str:\n17\t    return f\"{GROUNDED_PREFIX}seq={min_seq}\"\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tdef is_grounded(grounding: str | None) -> bool:\n21\t    return bool(grounding) and grounding.startswith(GROUNDED_PREFIX)\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tdef belief_mean(alpha: float, beta: float) -> float:\n25\t    a, b = max(1e-9, alpha), max(1e-9, beta)\n26\t    return a / (a + b)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef belief_lcb(alpha: float, beta: float, z: float = 1.0) -> float:\n30\t    \"\"\"Lower confidence bound = mean − z·sd of Beta(alpha,beta). A wide, low-support belief\n31\t    has a collapsed LCB even when its mean looks acceptable (the consistency.py insight).\"\"\"\n32\t    a, b = max(1e-9, alpha), max(1e-9, beta)\n33\t    mean = a / (a + b)\n34\t    var = (a * b) / ((a + b) ** 2 * (a + b + 1.0))\n35\t    return max(0.0, mean - z * math.sqrt(var))\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"a3768e61-b0e7-4b7d-b0bb-da11a96f8d55","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:44.546Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2d951e37-b3f9-4958-969c-8e5d52a0ce18","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"a3768e61-b0e7-4b7d-b0bb-da11a96f8d55","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9tCWDca3fKdB2XrGQaW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01T8eFbyyTHLHVv8nKc2uDad","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && echo \"===warden.rs 37-60===\"; sed -n '37,60p' kernel/src/warden.rs; echo \"===gate.py head===\"; sed -n '1,40p' sigil/consolidate/gate.py","description":"Read warden decide/record and gate veracity"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3572,"cache_read_input_tokens":56453,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3572,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9tCUEZLcvXi7La5mDLX","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"bbc03cf0-8459-4d71-a8d4-c9bf47d68d81","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:45.969Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"bbc03cf0-8459-4d71-a8d4-c9bf47d68d81","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01T8eFbyyTHLHVv8nKc2uDad","type":"tool_result","content":"===warden.rs 37-60===\n    pub fn decide(&self, tool: &str) -> Verdict {\n        let tier = self.registry.tier(tool);\n        Verdict { tier, decision: gate(tier) }\n    }\n\n    /// Append a signed action-log record for a completed (or blocked) invocation.\n    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n    pub fn record(\n        &self,\n        agent: &str,\n        tool: &str,\n        args_hash: &str,\n        verdict: &Verdict,\n        approver: &str,\n        result_hash: &str,\n        ts_epoch: u64,\n    ) -> io::Result<ActionRecord> {\n        self.log.append(\n            &self.key, agent, tool, args_hash,\n            verdict.tier.as_str(), verdict.decision.as_str(),\n            approver, result_hash, ts_epoch,\n        )\n    }\n\n===gate.py head===\n\"\"\"The veracity gate — SIGIL's demote-only admission choke point (ported from\nveracity/firewall.py's re-execute-don't-string-trust discipline).\n\nThe gate grounds the QUOTE, and the quote — the verbatim record span — is what becomes the\nserved fact. The model's free-text `statement` is NEVER the authoritative content (a\nred-team re-check proved a token-subset check over the statement is not entailment: an\nextractor can drop a negation or reorder words to invert meaning while staying a token\nsubset). By certifying and serving the verbatim quote instead, a fabricated or inverted\nstatement can never be presented as a grounded fact — the owner only ever sees their own words.\n\nA candidate GROUNDS iff:\n  1. it cites ≥1 seq, every cited seq is inside the exact window fed to the extractor;\n  2. its `quote` is verbatim in ≥1 cited record re-fetched from the spine (never the model's copy);\n  3. the quote is SPECIFIC — it carries ≥2 salient (non-stopword) tokens, Unicode-aware, so a\n     trivial common substring grounds nothing.\n\nThe gate can only ever DEMOTE; the model's confidence never enters. Anything that fails is\nrecorded honestly as commentary, never as a fact and never dropped.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom .grounding import UNGROUNDED, ground_tag\nfrom .models import CandidateFact, GateVerdict\n\n_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n# Unicode-aware: a token starts with a word char (letter/digit, any script) and may carry\n# internal separators (dates 2026-07-20, versions v1.2, paths a/b). \\w is Unicode by default.\n_TOKEN = re.compile(r\"[^\\W_][\\w'./-]*\", re.UNICODE)\n\n_STOPWORDS = frozenset({\n    \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"with\", \"that\", \"this\", \"have\", \"has\", \"had\", \"was\", \"were\", \"are\",\n    \"will\", \"would\", \"should\", \"could\", \"can\", \"but\", \"you\", \"your\", \"our\", \"their\",\n    \"from\", \"into\", \"out\", \"about\", \"then\", \"than\", \"them\", \"they\", \"there\", \"here\",\n    \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"which\", \"who\", \"how\", \"all\", \"any\", \"some\", \"get\", \"got\", \"let\",\n    \"let's\", \"lets\", \"actually\", \"just\", \"now\", \"today\", \"yesterday\", \"tomorrow\", \"really\",\n    \"very\", \"more\", \"most\", \"much\", \"many\", \"also\", \"like\", \"want\", \"need\", \"going\", \"gonna\",\n})","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"b4a1d9a2-cddf-4b0e-ad1d-8871dbb0cb38","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:02:49.803Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bbc03cf0-8459-4d71-a8d4-c9bf47d68d81","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"0aeeef14-2f99-46b5-86f2-2df5381166d8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"41d96f36-c558-41bc-8e41-725cff6d0f28","agentId":"a8fce3cb3495e5506","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KoPYbNdfU7rwLvDtSGHCpo","type":"tool_result","content":"===registry.rs===\n//! Per-tool tier registry (Phase 1.1) — the auditable complement to name-inference. A pin makes\n//! classification EXPLICIT for a named tool. Crucially it is **RAISE-ONLY**: the effective tier\n//! is `max(pin, inferred)`, so the override file (`~/.sigil/warden/tools.json`, plain text a\n//! file-write attacker could edit) can only ever make a tool MORE gated, never less — it can\n//! never downgrade `git.push` to auto. Legitimate de-escalation is intentionally NOT available\n//! via an unsigned file (that would need a signed mechanism; a future addition).\n\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\nuse crate::tiers::{classify, Tier};\n\npub struct Registry {\n    pins: HashMap<String, Tier>,\n}\n\nfn parse_tier(s: &str) -> Option<Tier> {\n    match s.trim().to_ascii_uppercase().as_str() {\n        \"A0\" => Some(Tier::A0),\n        \"A1\" => Some(Tier::A1),\n        \"A2\" => Some(Tier::A2),\n        \"A3\" => Some(Tier::A3),\n        _ => None,\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Registry {\n    pub fn empty() -> Self {\n        Registry { pins: HashMap::new() }\n    }\n\n    /// Load raise-only pins from a JSON object `{ \"tool.name\": \"A2\", ... }`. Unreadable/absent\n    /// file or malformed entries are ignored (the inference engine still governs). Never fails.\n    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Self {\n        let mut r = Registry::empty();\n        if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(path) {\n            if let Ok(map) = serde_json::from_str::<HashMap<String, String>>(&text) {\n                for (tool, tier_s) in map {\n                    if let Some(t) = parse_tier(&tier_s) {\n                        // case-colliding keys resolve to the HIGHER tier (deterministic +\n                        // raise-only-consistent), never last-writer-wins by hash order.\n                        let key = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n                        let eff = r.pins.get(&key).map_or(t, |&e| e.max(t));\n                        r.pins.insert(key, eff);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        r\n    }\n\n    /// The effective tier: `max(pin, inferred)`. A pin can only RAISE (never lower) the\n    /// token-inference result, so the override file is not a downgrade attack surface.\n    pub fn tier(&self, tool: &str) -> Tier {\n        let inferred = classify(tool);\n        match self.pins.get(&tool.to_ascii_lowercase()) {\n            Some(&pinned) => pinned.max(inferred),\n            None => inferred,\n        }\n    }\n===gate.py admit signature===\n25:from .models import CandidateFact, GateVerdict\n70:def admit(cand: CandidateFact, window_seqs: set[int], store: SpineStore) -> GateVerdict:\n75:        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [], \"cites no source records — cannot ground\")\n79:        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [],\n84:        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [], \"no quote to verify\")\n99:        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [],\n104:        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [],\n108:    return GateVerdict(True, ground_tag(min(vs)), vs,","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"69672f2a-43e4-44dd-8ca5-ef2653ac9a5c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T17:03:10.818Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0aeeef14-2f99-46b5-86f2-2df5381166d8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

A few verifications refined the design: the Rust `Registry::tier()` already composes `classify()` with raise-only pins (the exact reuse point for a classifier bridge), `cmd_do` in `main.rs` is a stub with no real executor, `gate.admit` enforces verbatim-quote + ≥2-salient-token grounding (the discipline both workstreams should mirror), and the frontier vision hop is genuinely data egress (must classify A2). Here is the design document.\n\n---\n\n# SIGIL — Build Plan: OPERATOR AGENT + LIVE VISION\n\n## 0. Framing: the one doctrine gap both workstreams must close\n\nThe substrate has a real seam the user named: **the Python `Governor` trusts `Proposal.tier`; the authoritative danger-classifier is Rust `tiers::classify` (danger-first, fail-closed A3), reachable only via the kernel CLI.** Today nothing in Python consults it. The peak move that lifts both workstreams above \"add a shell tool / add a VLM call\" is to make the Rust classifier the *tiering oracle* for the mesh — the Operator derives each step's tier from it (never self-declares), and Vision uses it to gate the frontier image-egress hop. This is the shared primitive; build it first.\n\nEvery slice below ends, per SIGIL's phase ritual, with: deterministic doubles + a real end-to-end proof + a red-pen pass + fixes before merge. Every new module lives under a package whose `__init__` already runs `assert_no_offense()` (`sigil/perception/__init__.py:11`, agents package), so offense-freeness is inherited structurally.\n\n---\n\n# WORKSTREAM 1 — OPERATOR AGENT\n\n## 1. Peak-innovative core mechanisms\n\n**M1 — The Rust classifier as the tiering oracle (closes the Python↔Rust WARDEN wiring gap).**\nThe Operator never sets `Proposal.tier` itself. For every concrete step it builds a *faithful tool token* (a projection of the real effect, e.g. `fs.read`, `fs.write`, `fs.delete`, `shell.exec.<argv0>`, `git.commit`, `git.push`) and asks the kernel for the authoritative tier. Because `classify` is danger-first and fail-closed, any op the Operator cannot map to a positively-safe token is automatically A3/queued. This converts \"Python trusts the declared tier\" into \"Python derives the tier from the fail-closed Rust oracle\" — the exact gap the user flagged, closed for the whole mesh.\n\n**M2 — Capability-scoped root sandbox (two rings).** A *read ring* (allowlisted roots the Operator may read) and a narrower *auto-write ring* (roots where an A1 reversible write may auto-apply). Every path is realpath-resolved and must be `is_relative_to` an allowed root; anything else is `_refuse()`'d and logged — never touched. This wraps `sources.read_source`'s UNGUARDED local-path branch (`sources.py:139`) so the Operator can never read an arbitrary path. Empty roots = deny-all (fail-closed), the inverse of the current unguarded default.\n\n**M3 — Plan → Preview(dry-run) → Approve → Execute → Verify, with a reversibility/undo ledger and transactional apply-or-rollback (the crown jewel).**\n- *Plan*: decompose a request into ordered typed `Step`s; each step's tier comes from M1; plan tier = max(step tiers).\n- *Preview*: compute a deterministic preview with **zero mutation** — a `difflib` unified diff for writes, a file+hash+size manifest for deletes, the exact rendered argv+cwd for shell (never executed). Reads may execute (A0) and are served as grounded facts (M4). The preview, including a **post-image hash** per step, is written to the spine.\n- *Approve*: the plan's A2/A3 steps are QUEUED through the existing Ed25519 `ApprovalQueue`. **Approval binds to the previewed content hash** — before execution the Operator re-hashes the plan and aborts on mismatch (anti-TOCTOU binding that does not exist anywhere in the repo today).\n- *Execute + undo ledger*: before each mutating step, capture the pre-image (bytes + sha256) into a per-transaction journal under `SIGIL_HOME` (0700), apply via atomic write-temp-then-rename or argv subprocess, then **VERIFY the re-read post-image hash equals the previewed hash** (prove-don't-guess applied to *actions* — success is proven by observing the changed world, not by a zero exit code). Any failure rolls back all applied steps in reverse via their recorded inverse ops. Every apply and every rollback is a signed, hash-chained spine record — the undo ledger *is* the spine. `undo(seq)` replays the recorded inverse and appends a superseding record.\n\n**M4 — Filesystem-reads-as-grounded-facts (serve-the-quote for the Operator).** A read serves the VERBATIM bytes as authoritative (reusing `compose_perception`'s column-0 `AUTHORITATIVE_HEADER` + `_GUARD` prefix discipline, `perceive.py:26-28`); any model summary is ADVISORY. A \"the file says X\" claim is only served if X is a verbatim substring (reuse the `gate.py` tokenizer + verbatim check). An operator that summarizes a config can never fabricate a value.\n\n**M5 — Reversibility as a *proven* property, making A1 \"reversible-auto\" honest.** A step rides A1-auto only if the Operator can synthesize and store an inverse (write with captured pre-image; delete with backup). A `shell.exec` of an arbitrary command has no synthesizable inverse → it is forced to A3 regardless of token and never auto-runs. Today the tier is *declared*; here reversibility is *proven by the existence of a captured inverse*. This is a genuine deepening of the WARDEN doctrine.\n\n## 2. Ordered TO-DO slices (thin, independently mergeable)\n\n**O1 — Classifier bridge (SHARED, build first).**\n- Modify `/home/kali/sigil/kernel/src/main.rs`: add `Cmd::Classify { tool }` + `cmd_classify` that returns `Registry::load(warden_dir()/\"tools.json\").tier(tool)` and `gate(tier)` as `A2 queued` / `--json`. **No action-log write** — classification is an A0 observation, not an action. Reuse `warden.rs::Registry`, `tiers::gate`.\n- New `/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/kernel_classify.py`: `KernelClassifier.classify(tool) -> agents.base.Tier`, shelling `subprocess.run([kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"])`. Reuse `voice/dispatch.py::_default_bin` + its error posture; **fail-closed to A3** on any error/empty output.\n\n**O2 — OperatorScope (capability-scoped sandbox).**\n- New `/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/operator_scope.py`: `OperatorScope(read_roots, auto_write_roots)`; `resolve(path, need) -> Optional[Path]` (realpath + `is_relative_to`). Reuse BASTION `self._allow`/`_refuse()` (`bastion.py:141,147`) for the allowlist + refusal record.\n\n**O3 — Grounded read (serve-the-quote).**\n- New `/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/operator.py` (start it here): `Operator(Agent)`, ceiling `A2`; method `read(path, question)` → resolve via O2, read, emit an `event` record with `captured_text` verbatim + `grounded=True` (mirror `perceive.py:82-86` payload shape), rendered with the authoritative/advisory boundary. Reuse `compose_perception` rendering + `gate.py` verbatim check. Wraps `sources.read_source` local branch behind the scope gate.\n\n**O4 — Plan + Preview (no mutation).**\n- Extend `operator.py`: `Step` dataclass; `plan(request)` (deterministic decomposition; an LLM proposer is injectable like ARTIFICER's `Coder` Protocol, but the plan is re-validated data); `preview(steps) -> PreviewReport` (difflib diff / delete manifest / rendered argv; reads execute A0). Each step tiered via O1; write the plan+previews as spine proposals via `_dispatch`. Reuse `difflib`, O1, O2.\n\n**O5 — Approve-binding + Execute + transactional rollback + verify + undo.**\n- Extend `operator.py`: `execute(plan_seq)` (re-hash + bind to approval, require VERIFIED `approvals.pending`/`verify_approval`), per-step pre-image journal, atomic apply, post-image verify, reverse rollback on failure; `undo(apply_seq)`. Reuse `agents/approvals.py` (the hardened signed queue), ARTIFICER's argv+timeout+`finally` cleanup (`artificer.py:99,117-119`), `store.append(...supersedes_id=...)` for undo supersession.\n\n**O6 — (Capstone, optional) real execution via the kernel action log.** Post-approval shell steps can additionally route through `sigil-kernel do <tool>` so the cross-anchored, anti-rollback **signed action log** records them (`main.rs::cmd_do`). Default remains Python-side argv exec within scope wrapped by the full preview/approve/verify loop.\n\n## 3. Test + real-proof strategy + red-pen lenses (per slice)\n\n- **O1**: double `FakeClassifier`; real proof shells the built kernel over a table (`git.push`→A3, `config.overwrite`→A3, `fs.write`→A1, `fs.read`→A0). *Red-pen*: classify must write NO action-log record; must reuse `Registry::tier` (not a reimplementation that could drift from the enforced path); error → A3.\n- **O2**: unit tests for `../`, symlink-out, absolute-outside → all refused; real proof builds a temp tree with a symlink escaping the root and asserts a `refusal` record. *Red-pen*: TOCTOU (open the *resolved* path, re-stat), case-insensitive FS, empty-roots = deny-all.\n- **O3**: read a fixture, assert served == verbatim bytes; assert a fabricated summary is labelled advisory; out-of-scope read refuses. *Red-pen*: binary/non-UTF8 → honest \"N bytes, sha256\" not garbage; truncation marked, never presented as complete; file content containing the header string cannot forge the boundary (the `REDPEN-P5-2` guard-prefix lesson).\n- **O4**: plan write+delete+shell; assert diffs correct, **disk untouched**, tiers correct (`shell.exec`→A3, delete→A3, write→A1). *Red-pen*: preview must never execute a side effect (including the shell \"preview\"); unclassifiable token → A3; post-image hash deterministic.\n- **O5**: 3-step transaction, step 3 fails → assert steps 1-2 rolled back byte-identical, ledger shows apply+rollback records; undo proof restores a write; TOCTOU proof mutates the plan record between preview and execute → execute aborts with a hash-mismatch refusal. *Red-pen*: an already-run irreversible shell step in a rolled-back transaction must report \"cannot fully roll back: step K irreversible\" (never a false success); journal pre-images may hold secrets → 0700, never egressed; approval replay blocked by the signed `target_seq` (RP-APPROVAL-4); verify uses the re-read post-image, not the exit code.\n\n## 4. Doctrine-safety analysis (WS1)\n\n- **Offense-free**: no exploit, no third-party target — operates only within owner-granted roots; new modules inherit `assert_no_offense()`; never `shell=True` (repo invariant preserved).\n- **Fail-closed**: unknown tokens → A3 (M1); empty scope → deny-all (M2); classifier/kernel error → A3; irreversible steps → A3, never auto (M5); approvals fail-closed to pending on any unsigned/forged/wrong-key approval (reused).\n- **Serve-the-quote**: reads serve verbatim bytes; command stdout is ADVISORY unless re-verified; success is proven by re-reading the world (M3 verify).\n- **Abuse vectors a reviewer will hunt**: token laundering (mitigated: token is a faithful projection + danger-first classifier + independent Python guard on destructive fs verbs + reversibility gate); scope escape via symlink/`..`/TOCTOU (realpath + resolved-path open); TOCTOU preview→execute (hash-bound approval); rollback dishonesty (partial-rollback honesty for irreversible steps); secret exposure in the journal (0700, no egress; secret-store paths classify A3 by the oracle); approval self-cert/replay (hardened signed queue reused).\n\n---\n\n# WORKSTREAM 2 — LIVE VISION\n\n## 1. Peak-innovative core mechanisms\n\n**V1 — Two real providers behind the existing `VisionModel` Protocol, local-first.** `MoondreamVision` (default) POSTs a base64 frame + question to Ollama `/api/generate` — reuse `extract.LocalProvider`'s urllib + `OLLAMA_HOST` pattern (`extract.py:175-198`); fully offline, nothing leaves the box. `ClaudeVision` (frontier) is completed from the `extract.ApiProvider` template (`extract.py:138-172`): base64 image content block → `/v1/messages`, `x-api-key` from `SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`.
